taina  samayaina cūḷasumanatthairau nāma sabbapariyattidharau vinayācariyapāmaukkhau mahāvihārai paṭivasati| sau bhikkhu thairaṃ upasaṅkamitvā vanditvā aukāsaṃ kāraitvā attanau kuk‍kuc‍caṃ pucchi| thairau taina bhaṭṭhai janakāyai pacchā āgantvā gahitabhāvaṃ ñatvā ‘‘atthi dāni aittha aukāsau’’ti cintaitvā āha ‘‘sacai kāsāvasāmikaṃ bhikkhuṃ ānaiyyāsi, sak‍kā bhavaiyya tava patiṭṭhā kātu’’nti| kathāhaṃ, bhantai, taṃ dakkhissāmīti| tahiṃ tahiṃ gantvā aulaukaihīti| sau pañ‍capi mahāvihārai aulaukaitvā naiva addakkhi| tatau naṃ thairau pucchi ‘‘katarāya disāya bahū bhikkhū āgacchantī’’ti? ‘‘dakkhiṇadisāya, bhantai’’ti| taina hi kāsāvaṃ dīghatau ca tiriyañ‍ca minitvā ṭhapaihi, ṭhapaitvā dakkhiṇadisāya vihārapaṭipāṭiyā vicinitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ ānaihīti| sau tathā katvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā thairassa santikaṃ ānaisi| thairau pucchi ‘‘tavaidaṃ kāsāva’’nti? ‘‘āma, bhantai’’ti| kuhiṃ tai pātitanti? sau sabbaṃ ācikkhi| thairau taina kataṃ dhuranikkhaipaṃ sutvā itaraṃ pucchi ‘‘tayā idaṃ kuhiṃ disvā gahita’’nti? saupi sabbaṃ āraucaisi| tatau taṃ thairau āha ‘‘sacai tai suddhacittaina gahitaṃ abhavissa, anāpattiyaiva tai assa, thaiyyacittaina pana gahitattā duk‍kaṭaṃ āpan‍nausi, taṃ daisaitvā anāpattikau hauti, idañ‍ca kāsāvaṃ attanau santakaṃ katvā aitassaiva bhikkhunau daihī’’ti| sau bhikkhu amatainaiva abhisittau varamassāsappattau ahausi| aivaṃ vatthu aulaukaitabbaṃ|

kālauti avahārakālau| tadaiva hi bhaṇḍaṃ kadāci appagghaṃ hauti, kadāci mahagghaṃ| tasmā taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ yasmiṃ kālai avahaṭaṃ, tasmiṃyaiva kālai yau tassa agghau hauti, taina agghaina āpatti kāraitabbā| aivaṃ kālau aulaukaitabbau|

daisauti avahāradaisau| tañhi bhaṇḍaṃ yasmiṃ daisai avahaṭaṃ, tasmiṃyaiva daisai yau tassa agghau hauti, taina agghaina āpatti kāraitabbā| bhaṇḍuṭṭhānadaisai hi bhaṇḍaṃ appagghaṃ hauti, añ‍ñattha mahagghaṃ|

imassapi  ca atthassa dīpanatthamidaṃ vatthu – antarasamuddai kira aikau bhikkhu susaṇṭhānaṃ nāḷikairaṃ labhitvā bhamaṃ āraupaitvā saṅkhathālakasadisaṃ manauramaṃ pānīyathālakaṃ katvā tatthaiva ṭhapaitvā caitiyagiriṃ agamāsi| añ‍ñau bhikkhu antarasamuddaṃ gantvā tasmiṃ vihārai paṭivasantau taṃ thālakaṃ disvā thaiyyacittaina gahaitvā caitiyagirimaiva āgatau| tassa tattha yāguṃ pivantassa taṃ thālakaṃ disvā thālakasāmikau bhikkhu āha ‘‘kutau tai idaṃ laddha’’nti| antarasamuddatau mai ānītanti| sau taṃ ‘‘naitaṃ tava santakaṃ, thaiyyāya tai gahita’’nti saṅghamajjhaṃ ākaḍḍhi| tattha ca vinicchayaṃ alabhitvā mahāvihāraṃ agamiṃsu, tattha ca bhairiṃ paharāpaitvā mahācaitiyasamīpai san‍nipātaṃ katvā vinicchayaṃ ārabhiṃsu| vinayadharatthairā avahāraṃ sañ‍ñāpaisuṃ|

tasmiñ‍ca san‍nipātai ābhidhammikagaudattatthairau nāma vinayakusalau hauti, sau aivamāha ‘‘iminā idaṃ thālakaṃ kuhiṃ avahaṭa’’nti? ‘‘antarasamuddai avahaṭa’’nti| tattha taṃ kiṃ agghatīti| na kiñ‍ci agghati| tatra hi nāḷikairaṃ bhinditvā miñ‍jaṃ khāditvā kapālaṃ chaḍḍaiti, dāruatthaṃ pana pharatīti| imassa bhikkhunau aittha hatthakammaṃ kiṃ agghatīti? māsakaṃ vā ūnamāsakaṃ vāti| atthi pana katthaci sammāsambuddhaina māsakai vā ūnamāsakai vā pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattanti| aivaṃ vuttai ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sukathitaṃ suvinicchita’’nti aikasādhukārau ahausi| taina ca samayaina bhātiyarājāpi caitiyavandanatthaṃ nagaratau nikkhantau taṃ saddaṃ sutvā ‘‘kiṃ ida’’nti pucchitvā sabbaṃ paṭipāṭiyā sutvā nagarai bhairiṃ carāpaisi ‘‘mayi santai bhikkhūnampi bhikkhunīnampi gihīnampi adhikaraṇaṃ ābhidhammikagaudattatthairaina vinicchitaṃ suvinicchitaṃ, tassa vinicchayai atiṭṭhamānaṃ rājāṇāya ṭhapaimī’’ti| aivaṃ daisau aulaukaitabbau|

agghauti bhaṇḍagghau| navabhaṇḍassa hi yau agghau hauti, sau pacchā parihāyati| yathā navadhautau pattau aṭṭha vā dasa vā agghati, sau pacchā bhin‍nau vā chiddau vā āṇigaṇṭhikāhatau vā appagghau hauti, tasmā na sabbadā bhaṇḍaṃ pakatiagghainaiva kātabbanti| aivaṃ agghau aulaukaitabbau|

paribhaugauti bhaṇḍaparibhaugau| paribhaugainapi hi vāsiādibhaṇḍassa agghau parihāyati| tasmā aivaṃ upaparikkhitabbaṃ – sacai kauci kassaci pādagghanakaṃ vāsiṃ harati, tatra vāsisāmikau pucchitabbau ‘‘tayā ayaṃ vāsi kittakaina kītā’’ti? ‘‘pādaina, bhantai’’ti| kiṃ pana tai kiṇitvāva ṭhapitā, udāhu naṃ vaḷañ‍jaisīti? sacai vadati ‘‘aikadivasaṃ mai dantakaṭṭhaṃ  vā rajanachal‍li vā pattapacanakadāru vā chin‍naṃ, ghaṃsitvā vā nisitā’’ti, athassa paurāṇakau  agghau bhaṭṭhauti vaiditabbau| yathā ca vāsiyā, aivaṃ añ‍janiyā vā añ‍janisalākāya vā kuñ‍cikāya vā palālaina vā thusaihi vā iṭṭhakacuṇṇaina vā aikavāraṃ ghaṃsitvā dhauvitamattainapi agghau bhassati| tipumaṇḍalassa makaradantacchaidanainapi parimaj‍janamattainapi, udakasāṭakassa sakiṃ nivāsanapārupanainapi paribhaugasīsaina aṃsai vā sīsai vā ṭhapanamattainapi, taṇḍulādīnaṃ papphauṭanainapi tatau aikaṃ vā dvai vā apanayanainapi antamasau aikaṃ pāsāṇasakkharaṃ uddharitvā chaḍḍitamattainapi, sappitailādīnaṃ bhājanantarapaavattanainapi antamasau tatau makkhikaṃ vā kipil‍likaṃ vā uddharitvā chaḍḍitamattainapi, guḷapiṇḍakassa madhurabhāvajānanatthaṃ nakhaina vijjhitvā aṇumattaṃ gahitamattainapi agghau bhassati| tasmā yaṃ kiñ‍ci pādagghanakaṃ vuttanayainaiva sāmikaihi paribhaugaina ūnaṃ kataṃ hauti, na taṃ avahaṭau bhikkhu pārājikaina kātabbauti| aivaṃ agghau aulaukaitabbau|

aivaṃ imāni tulayitvā pañ‍ca ṭhānāni dhāraiyya atthaṃ vicakkhaṇau, āpattiṃ vā anāpattiṃ vā garukaṃ vā lahukaṃ vā āpattiṃ yathāṭhānai ṭhapaiyyāti| taināhu aṭṭhakathācariyā –

‘‘sikkhāpadaṃ samaṃ taina, añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci na vij‍jati|

anaikanayavaukiṇṇaṃ, gambhīratthavinicchayaṃ||

‘‘tasmā vatthumhi autiṇṇai, bhikkhunā vinayañ‍ñunā|

vinayānuggahainaittha, karauntaina vinicchayaṃ||

‘‘pāḷiṃ aṭṭhakathañ‍caiva, sādhippāyamasaisatau|

augayha appamattaina, karaṇīyau vinicchayau||

‘‘āpattidassanussāhau, na kattabbau kudācanaṃ|

passissāmi anāpatti-miti kayirātha mānasaṃ||

‘‘passitvāpi ca āpattiṃ, avatvāva punappunaṃ|

vīmaṃsitvātha viñ‍ñūhi, saṃsanditvā ca taṃ vadai||

‘‘kappiyaipi  ca vatthusmiṃ, cittassa lahuvattinau|

vasaina sāmañ‍ñaguṇā, cavantīdha puthuj‍janā||

‘‘tasmā paraparikkhāraṃ, āsīvisamivauragaṃ|

aggiṃ viya ca sampassaṃ, nāmasaiyya vicakkhaṇau’’ti|| (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.160-1 tatrāyaṃ anusāsanī)|

233. uttarimanussadhammāraucanaṃ vinicchinantaina (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.197) pana ‘‘kiṃ tai adhigataṃ| kinti tai adhigataṃ, kadā tai adhigataṃ, kattha tai adhigataṃ, katamai tai kilaisā pahīnā, katamaisaṃ tvaṃ dhammānaṃ lābhī’’ti imāni cha ṭhānāni visaudhaitabbāni| sacai hi kauci bhikkhu uttarimanussadhammādhigamaṃ byākaraiyya, na sau aittāvatā sak‍kārau kātabbau, imaisaṃ pana chan‍naṃ ṭhānānaṃ saudhanatthaṃ aivaṃ vattabbau ‘‘kiṃ tai adhigataṃ, kiṃ jhānaṃ udāhu vimaukkhādīsu añ‍ñatara’’nti| yau hi yaina adhigatau dhammau, sau tassa pākaṭau hauti| sacai ‘‘idaṃ nāma mai adhigata’’nti vadati, tatau ‘‘kinti tai adhigata’’nti pucchitabbau, ‘‘anic‍calakkhaṇādīsu kiṃ dhuraṃ katvā aṭṭhatiṃsāya vā ārammaṇaisu rūpārūpaajjhattabahiddhādibhaidaisu vā dhammaisu kaina mukhaina abhinivisitvā’’ti| yau hi yassābhinivaisau, sau tassa pākaṭau hauti| sacai ‘‘ayaṃ nāma mai abhinivaisau, aivaṃ mayā adhigata’’nti vadati, tatau ‘‘kadā tai adhigata’’nti pucchitabbau, ‘‘kiṃ pubbaṇhai, udāhu majjhanhikādīsu añ‍ñatarasmiṃ kālai’’ti| sabbaisañhi attanā adhigatakālau pākaṭau hauti| sacai ‘‘amukasmiṃ nāma kālai adhigaka’’nti vadati, tatau ‘‘kattha tai adhigata’’nti pucchitabbau, ‘‘kiṃ divāṭṭhānai, udāhu rattiṭṭhānādīsu añ‍ñatarasmiṃ aukāsai’’ti| sabbaisañhi attanā adhigataukāsau pākaṭau hauti| sacai ‘‘amukasmiṃ nāma mai aukāsai adhigata’’nti vadati, tatau ‘‘katamai tai kilaisā pahīnā’’ti pucchitabbau, ‘‘kiṃ paṭhamamaggavajjhā, udāhu dutiyādimaggavajjhā’’ti| sabbaisañhi attanā adhigatamaggaina pahīnā kilaisā pākaṭā haunti|

sacai ‘‘imai nāma mai kilaisā pahīnā’’ti vadati, tatau ‘‘katamaisaṃ tvaṃ dhammānaṃ lābhī’’ti pucchitabbau, ‘‘kiṃ sautāpattimaggassa, udāhu sakadāgāmimaggādīsu añ‍ñatarassā’’ti| sabbaisañhi attanā adhigatadhammau pākaṭau  hauti| sacai ‘‘imaisaṃ nāmāhaṃ dhammānaṃ lābhī’’ti vadati, aittāvatāpissa vacanaṃ na saddhātabbaṃ| bahussutā hi uggahaparipucchākusalā bhikkhū imāni cha ṭhānāni saudhaituṃ sak‍kaunti, imassa pana bhikkhunau āgamanapaṭipadā  saudhaitabbā| yadi āgamanapaṭipadā na sujjhati, ‘‘imāya paṭipadāya laukuttaradhammau nāma na labbhatī’’ti apanaitabbau| yadi panassa āgamanapaṭipadā sujjhati, dīgharattaṃ tīsu sikkhāsu appamattau jāgariyamanuyuttau catūsu pac‍cayaisu alaggau ākāsai pāṇisamaina caitasā viharatīti pañ‍ñāyati, tassa bhikkhunau byākaraṇaṃ paṭipadāya saddhiṃ saṃsandati| ‘‘saiyyathāpi nāma gaṅgaudakaṃ yamunaudakaina saṃsandati samaiti, aivamaiva supañ‍ñattā taina bhagavatā sāvakānaṃ nibbānagāminī paṭipadā, saṃsandati nibbānañ‍ca paṭipadā cā’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.296) vuttasadisaṃ hauti| apica khau na aittakainapi sak‍kārau kātabbau| kasmā? aikac‍cassa hi puthuj‍janassapi satau khīṇāsavassa paṭipattisadisā paṭipatti hauti, tasmā sau bhikkhu taihi taihi upāyaihi uttāsaitabbau| khīṇāsavassa nāma asaniyāpi matthakai patamānāya bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā na hauti, puthuj‍janassa appamattakainapi hauti|

tatrimāni vatthūni (ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 3.102) – dīghabhāṇakaabhayatthairau kira aikaṃ piṇḍapātikaṃ pariggahaituṃ asak‍kauntau daharassa sañ‍ñaṃ adāsi| sau taṃ nahāyamānaṃ kalyāṇīnadīmukhadvārai nimuj‍jitvā pādai aggahaisi| piṇḍapātikau ‘‘kumbhīlau’’ti sañ‍ñāya mahāsaddamakāsi, tadā naṃ ‘‘puthuj‍janau’’ti jāniṃsu|

candamukhatissarājakālai pana mahāvihārai saṅghatthairau khīṇāsavau dubbalacakkhukau vihāraiyaiva acchati| rājā ‘‘thairaṃ pariggaṇhissāmī’’ti bhikkhūsu bhikkhācāraṃ gataisu appasaddau upasaṅkamitvā sappau viya pādai aggahaisi| thairau silāthambhau viya nic‍calau hutvā ‘‘kau aitthā’’ti āha| ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, tissau’’ti? ‘‘sugandhaṃ vāyasi nau tissā’’ti| aivaṃ khīṇāsavassa bhayaṃ nāma natthi|

aikac‍cau pana puthuj‍janaupi atisūrau hauti nibbhayau| sau rajanīyaina ārammaṇaina pariggaṇhitabbau| vasabharājāpi aikaṃ thairaṃ pariggaṇhamānau gharai nisīdāpaitvā tassa santikai badarasāḷavaṃ maddamānau  nisīdi| mahāthairassa khaiḷau calitau, thairassa puthuj‍janabhāvau āvibhūtau| khīṇāsavassa hi rasataṇhā nāma suppahīnā, dibbaisupi rasaisu nikanti nāma na hauti, tasmā imaihi upāyaihi pariggahaitvā sacassa bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā rasataṇhā vā uppaj‍jati, ‘‘na ca tvaṃ arahā’’ti apanaitabbau| sacai pana abhīru acchambhī anutrāsī hutvā sīhau viya nisīdati, dibbārammaṇaipi nikantiṃ na janaiti, ayaṃ bhikkhu sampan‍navaiyyākaraṇau  samantā rājarājamahāmattādīhi paisitaṃ sak‍kāraṃ arahatīti vaiditabbau| aivaṃ tāva uttarimanussadhammāraucanaṃ vinicchinitabbaṃ|

234. sakalai pana vinayavinicchayai (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.45) kausal‍laṃ patthayantaina catubbidhau vinayau jānitabbau|

catubbidhañhi vinayaṃ, mahāthairā mahiddhikā|

nīharitvā pakāsaisuṃ, dhammasaṅgāhakā purā||

katamaṃ catubbidhaṃ? suttaṃ suttānulaumaṃ ācariyavādaṃ attanaumatinti| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘āhac‍capadaina khau, mahārāja, rasaina ācariyavaṃsaina adhippāyā’’ti (mi॰ pa॰ 4.2.3)| aittha hi āhac‍capadanti suttaṃ adhippaitaṃ| rasauti suttānulaumaṃ| ācariyavaṃsauti ācariyavādau| adhippāyauti attanaumati|

tattha suttaṃ nāma sakalavinayapiṭakai pāḷi|

suttānulaumaṃ nāma cattārau mahāpadaisā| yai bhagavatā aivaṃ vuttā –

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ‘idaṃ na kappatī’ti appaṭikkhittaṃ, tañ‍cai akappiyaṃ anulaumaiti, kappiyaṃ paṭibāhati, taṃ vau na kappati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ‘idaṃ na kappatī’ti appaṭikkhittaṃ, tañ‍cai kappiyaṃ anulaumaiti, akappiyaṃ paṭibāhati, taṃ vau kappati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ‘idaṃ kappatī’ti ananuñ‍ñātaṃ, taṃ cai akappiyaṃ anulaumaiti, kappiyaṃ paṭibāhati, taṃ vau na kappati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ‘idaṃ kappatī’ti ananuñ‍ñātaṃ, tañ‍cai kappiyaṃ anulaumaiti, akappiyaṃ paṭibāhati, taṃ vau kappatī’’ti (mahāva॰ 305)|

ācariyavādau  nāma dhammasaṅgāhakaihi pañ‍cahi arahantasataihi ṭhapitā pāḷivinimuttā auk‍kantavinicchayappavattā aṭṭhakathātanti|

attanaumati  nāma suttasuttānulaumaācariyavādai muñ‍citvā anumānaina attanau anubuddhiyā nayaggāhaina upaṭṭhitākārakathanaṃ|

apica suttantābhidhammavinayaṭṭhakathāsu āgatau sabbaupi thairavādau attanaumati nāma| taṃ pana attanaumatiṃ gahaitvā kathaintaina na daḷhaggāhaṃ gahaitvā vauharitabbaṃ, kāraṇaṃ sal‍lakkhaitvā atthaina pāḷiṃ, pāḷiyā ca atthaṃ saṃsanditvā kathaitabbaṃ, attanaumati ācariyavādai autāraitabbā| sacai tattha autarati caiva samaiti ca, gahaitabbā| sacai naiva autarati na samaiti, na gahaitabbā| ayañhi attanaumati nāma sabbadubbalā, attanaumatitau ācariyavādau balavatarau|

ācariyavādaupi suttānulaumai autāraitabbau| tattha autarantau samaintau aiva gahaitabbau, itarau na gahaitabbau| ācariyavādatau hi suttānulaumaṃ balavataraṃ|

suttānulaumampi suttai autāraitabbaṃ| tattha autarantaṃ samaintamaiva gahaitabbaṃ, itaraṃ na gahaitabbaṃ| suttānulaumatau hi suttamaiva balavataraṃ| suttañhi appaṭivattiyaṃ kārakasaṅghasadisaṃ buddhānaṃ ṭhitakālasadisaṃ| tasmā yadā dvai bhikkhū sākacchanti, sakavādī suttaṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, paravādī suttānulaumaṃ| taihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ khaipaṃ vā garahaṃ vā akatvā suttānulaumaṃ suttai autāraitabbaṃ| sacai autarati samaiti, gahaitabbaṃ, nau cai, na gahaitabbaṃ, suttasmiṃyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ| athāyaṃ suttaṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau ācariyavādaṃ| taihipi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ khaipaṃ vā garahaṃ vā akatvā ācariyavādau suttai autāraitabbau| sacai autarati samaiti, gahaitabbau| anautarantau asamaintau ca gārayhācariyavādau na gahaitabbau, suttasmiṃyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ| athāyaṃ suttaṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau attanaumatiṃ| taihipi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ khaipaṃ vā garahaṃ vā akatvā attanaumati suttai autāraitabbā| sacai autarati samaiti, gahaitabbā, nau cai, na gahaitabbā, suttasmiṃyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ|

athāyaṃ  suttānulaumaṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau suttaṃ, suttānulaumai autāraitabbaṃ| sacai autarati samaiti, tissau saṅgītiyau āruḷhaṃ pāḷiāgataṃ pañ‍ñāyati, gahaitabbaṃ, nau cai tathā pañ‍ñāyati, na autarati na samaiti, bāhirakasuttaṃ vā hauti silaukau vā añ‍ñaṃ vā gārayhasuttaṃ guḷhavaissantaraguḷhavinayavaidal‍lādīnaṃ añ‍ñataratau ābhataṃ, na gahaitabbaṃ, suttānulaumasmiṃyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ| athāyaṃ suttānulaumaṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau ācariyavādaṃ| ācariyavādau  suttānulaumai autāraitabbau| sacai autarati samaiti, gahaitabbau| nau cai, na gahaitabbau, suttānulaumaiyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ| athāyaṃ suttānulaumaṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau attanaumatiṃ| attanaumati suttānulaumai autāraitabbā| sacai autarati samaiti, gahaitabbā| nau cai, na gahaitabbā, suttānulaumaiyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ|

athāyaṃ ācariyavādaṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau suttaṃ| suttaṃ ācariyavādai autāraitabbaṃ| sacai autarati samaiti, gahaitabbaṃ| itaraṃ gārayhasuttaṃ na gahaitabbaṃ, ācariyavādaiyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ| athāyaṃ ācariyavādaṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau suttānulaumaṃ| suttānulaumaṃ ācariyavādai autāraitabbaṃ| autarantaṃ samaintamaiva gahaitabbaṃ, itaraṃ na gahaitabbaṃ, ācariyavādaiyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ| athāyaṃ ācariyavādaṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau attanaumatiṃ| attanaumati ācariyavādai autāraitabbā| sacai autarati samaiti, gahaitabbā| nau cai, na gahaitabbā, ācariyavādaiyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ|

atha panāyaṃ attanaumatiṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau suttaṃ| suttaṃ attanaumatiyaṃ autāraitabbaṃ| sacai autarati samaiti, gahaitabbaṃ| itaraṃ gārayhasuttaṃ na gahaitabbaṃ, attanaumatiyamaiva ṭhātabbaṃ| athāyaṃ attanaumatiṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau suttānulaumaṃ| suttānulaumaṃ attanaumatiyaṃ autāraitabbaṃ| autarantaṃ samaintamaiva gahaitabbaṃ, itaraṃ na gahaitabbaṃ, attanaumatiyamaiva ṭhātabbaṃ| athāyaṃ attanaumatiṃ gahaitvā kathaiti, parau ācariyavādaṃ| ācariyavādau attanaumatiyaṃ autāraitabbau| sacai autarati samaiti, gahaitabbau| itarau gārayhācariyavādau na gahaitabbau, attanaumatiyamaiva  ṭhātabbaṃ, attanau gahaṇamaiva baliyaṃ kātabbaṃ| sabbaṭṭhānaisu ca khaipau vā garahā vā na kātabbāti|

atha panāyaṃ kappiyanti gahaitvā kathaiti, parau akappiyanti, suttai ca suttānulaumai ca autāraitabbaṃ| sacai kappiyaṃ hauti, kappiyai ṭhātabbaṃ| sacai akappiyaṃ, akappiyai ṭhātabbaṃ| athāyaṃ tassa kappiyabhāvasādhakaṃ suttatau bahuṃ kāraṇañ‍ca vinicchayañ‍ca dassaiti, parau kāraṇaṃ na vindati, kappiyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ| atha parau tassa akappiyabhāvasādhakaṃ suttatau bahuṃ kāraṇañ‍ca vinicchayañ‍ca dassaiti, anaina attanau gahaṇanti katvā daḷhaṃ ādāya na ṭhātabbaṃ, ‘‘sādhū’’ti sampaṭicchitvā akappiyai aiva ṭhātabbaṃ| atha dvin‍nampi kāraṇacchāyā dissati, paṭikkhittabhāvauyaiva sādhu, akappiyai ṭhātabbaṃ| vinayañhi patvā kappiyākappiyavicāraṇaṃ āgamma rundhitabbaṃ, gāḷhaṃ kattabbaṃ, sautaṃ pacchinditabbaṃ, garukabhāvaiyaiva ṭhātabbaṃ|

atha  panāyaṃ akappiyanti gahaitvā kathaiti, parau kappiyanti, suttai ca suttānulaumai ca autāraitabbaṃ| sacai kappiyaṃ hauti, kappiyai ṭhātabbaṃ| sacai akappiyaṃ, akappiyai ṭhātabbaṃ| athāyaṃ bahūhi suttavinicchayakāraṇaihi akappiyabhāvaṃ dassaiti, parau kāraṇaṃ na vindati, akappiyai ṭhātabbaṃ| atha parau bahūhi suttavinicchayakāraṇaihi kappiyabhāvaṃ dassaiti, ayaṃ kāraṇaṃ na vindati, kappiyai ṭhātabbaṃ| atha dvin‍nampi kāraṇacchāyā dissati, attanau gahaṇaṃ na vissaj‍jaitabbaṃ| yathā cāyaṃ kappiyākappiyai akappiyakappiyai ca vinicchayau vuttau, aivaṃ anāpattiāpattivādai āpattānāpattivādai ca, lahukagarukāpattivādai garukalahukāpattivādai cāpi vinicchayau vaiditabbau| nāmamattaiyaiva hi aittha nānaṃ, yaujanānayai nānaṃ natthi, tasmā na vitthāritaṃ|

aivaṃ kappiyākappiyādivinicchayai uppan‍nai yau suttasuttānulaumaācariyavādaattanaumatīsu atiraikakāraṇaṃ labhati, tassa vādai ṭhātabbaṃ, sabbasau pana kāraṇavinicchayaṃ alabhantaina suttaṃ na jahitabbaṃ, suttasmiṃyaiva ṭhātabbanti| aivaṃ sakalavinayavinicchayai kausal‍laṃ patthayantaina ayaṃ catubbidhau vinayau jānitabbau|

imañ‍ca pana catubbidhaṃ vinayaṃ ñatvāpi vinayadharaina puggalaina tilakkhaṇasaman‍nāgataina bhavitabbaṃ| tīṇi hi vinayadharassa lakkhaṇāni icchitabbāni| katamāni  tīṇi? suttañ‍cassa svāgataṃ hauti suppavatti suvinicchitaṃ suttatau anubyañ‍janasauti idamaikaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ| vinayai khau pana ṭhitau hauti asaṃhīrauti idaṃ dutiyaṃ| ācariyaparamparā khau panassa suggahitā hauti sumanasikatā sūpadhāritāti idaṃ tatiyaṃ|

tattha suttaṃ nāma sakalaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃ| tadassa svāgataṃ hautīti suṭṭhu āgataṃ| suppavattīti suṭṭhu pavattaṃ paguṇaṃ vācuggataṃ| suvinicchitaṃ suttasau anubyañ‍janasauti pāḷitau ca paripucchatau ca aṭṭhakathātau ca suvinicchitaṃ hauti kaṅkhāchaidanaṃ katvā uggahitaṃ| vinayai khau pana ṭhitau hautīti vinayai laj‍jibhāvaina patiṭṭhitau hauti| alaj‍jī hi bahussutaupi samānau lābhagarukatāya tantiṃ visaṃvādaitvā uddhammaṃ ubbinayaṃ satthusāsanaṃ dīpaitvā sāsanai mahantaṃ upaddavaṃ karauti, saṅghabhaidampi saṅgharājimpi uppādaiti| laj‍jī pana kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau jīvitahaitupi tantiṃ avisaṃvādaitvā dhammamaiva vinayamaiva dīpaiti, satthusāsanaṃ garukaṃ katvā ṭhapaiti| tathā hi pubbai mahāthairā tikkhattuṃ vācaṃ nicchāraisuṃ ‘‘anāgatai laj‍jī rakkhissati, laj‍jī  rakkhissati, laj‍jī rakkhissatī’’ti (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.45)| aivaṃ yau laj‍jī, sau vinayaṃ avijahantau avauk‍kamantau laj‍jibhāvainaiva vinayai ṭhitau hauti suppatiṭṭhitauti|

asaṃhīrauti saṃhīrau nāma yau pāḷiyaṃ vā aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vā haiṭṭhatau vā uparitau vā padapaṭipāṭiyā vā pucchiyamānau vitthunati vipphandati santiṭṭhituṃ na sak‍kauti, yaṃ yaṃ paraina vuc‍cati, taṃ taṃ anujānāti, sakavādaṃ chaḍḍaitvā paravādaṃ gaṇhāti| yau pana pāḷiyaṃ vā aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vā haiṭṭhupariyaina vā padapaṭipāṭiyā vā pucchiyamānau na vitthunati na vipphandati, aikaikalaumaṃ saṇḍāsaina gaṇhantau viya ‘‘aivaṃ mayaṃ vadāma, aivaṃ nau ācariyā vadantī’’ti vissaj‍jaiti, yamhi pāḷi ca pāḷivinicchayau ca suvaṇṇabhājanai pakkhittasīhavasā viya parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ agacchantau tiṭṭhati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘asaṃhīrau’’ti|

ācariyaparamparā khau panassa suggahitā hautīti thairaparamparā vaṃsaparamparā assa suṭṭhu gahitā hauti| sumanasikatāti suṭṭhu manasikatā, āvaj‍jitamattai uj‍jalitapadīpau viya hauti| sūpadhāritāti suṭṭhu upadhāritā pubbāparānusandhitau atthatau kāraṇatau ca upadhāritā| attanaumatiṃ pahāya ācariyasuddhiyā vattā hauti, ‘‘mayhaṃ ācariyau asukācariyassa santikai uggaṇhi, sau asukassā’’ti aivaṃ sabbaṃ ācariyaparamparaṃ thairavādaṅgaṃ haritvā yāva upālitthairau sammāsambuddhassa santikai uggaṇhīti pāpaitvā ṭhapaiti, tataupi āharitvā upālitthairau sammāsambuddhassa santikai uggaṇhi, dāsakatthairau attanau upajjhāyassa upālitthairassa, sauṇatthairau attanau upajjhāyassa dāsakatthairassa, siggavatthairau attanau upajjhāyassa sauṇatthairassa, mauggaliputtatissatthairau attanau upajjhāyassa siggavatthairassa caṇḍavaj‍jitthairassa cāti aivaṃ sabbaṃ ācariyaparamparaṃ thairavādaṅgaṃ āharitvā attanau ācariyaṃ pāpaitvā ṭhapaiti| aivaṃ uggahitā hi ācariyaparamparā suggahitā hauti| aivaṃ asak‍kauntaina pana avassaṃ dvai tayau parivaṭṭā uggahaitabbā| sabbapacchimaina hi nayaina yathā ācariyau ācariyācariyau ca pāḷiñ‍ca paripucchañ‍ca vadanti, tathā ñātuṃ vaṭṭati|

imaihi ca pana tīhi lakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgataina vinayadharaina vatthuvinicchayatthaṃ san‍nipatitai saṅghai autiṇṇai vatthusmiṃ caudakaina ca cuditakaina ca vuttai vattabbai sahasā avinicchinitvāva cha ṭhānāni aulaukaitabbāni| katamāni cha? vatthu aulaukaitabbaṃ, mātikā aulaukaitabbā, padabhājanīyaṃ aulaukaitabbaṃ, tikaparicchaidau aulaukaitabbau, antarāpatti aulaukaitabbā, anāpatti aulaukaitabbāti|

vatthuṃ  aulaukaintaupi hi ‘‘tiṇaina vā paṇṇaina vā paṭicchādaitvā āgantabbaṃ, na tvaiva naggaina āgantabbaṃ, yau āgacchaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti (pārā॰ 517) aivaṃ aikac‍caṃ āpattiṃ passati, sau taṃ suttaṃ ānaitvā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaissati|

mātikaṃ aulaukaintaupi ‘‘sampajānamusāvādai pācittiya’’ntiādinā (pāci॰ 3) nayaina pañ‍can‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ āpattiṃ passati, sau taṃ suttaṃ ānaitvā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaissati|

padabhājanīyaṃ aulaukaintaupi ‘‘akkhayitai sarīrai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, āpatti pārājikassa| yaibhuyyaina khayitai sarīrai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭisaivati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’tiādinā (pārā॰ 59 atthatau samānaṃ) nayaina sattan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ āpattiṃ passati, sau padabhājanīyatau suttaṃ ānaitvā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaissati|

tikaparicchaidaṃ aulaukaintaupi tikasaṅghādisaisaṃ vā tikapācittiyaṃ vā tikaduk‍kaṭaṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā āpattiṃ tikaparicchaidai passati, sau tatau suttaṃ ānaitvā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaissati|

antarāpattiṃ aulaukaintaupi ‘‘paṭilātaṃ ukkhipati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti (pāci॰ 355) aivaṃ yā sikkhāpadantaraisu antarāpatti hauti, taṃ passati, sau taṃ suttaṃ ānaitvā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaissati|

anāpattiṃ aulaukaintaupi ‘‘anāpatti bhikkhu asādiyantassa, athaiyyacittassa, na maraṇādhippāyassa, anul‍lapanādhippāyassa, na maucanādhippāyassa asañ‍cic‍ca asatiyā ajānantassā’’ti (pārā॰ 72, 136, 180, 225, 263 thaukaṃ thaukaṃ visadisaṃ) aivaṃ tasmiṃ tasmiṃ sikkhāpadai niddiṭṭhaṃ anāpattiṃ passati, sau taṃ suttaṃ ānaitvā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaissati|

yau hi bhikkhu catubbidhavinayakauvidau tilakkhaṇasampan‍nau imāni cha ṭhānāni aulaukaitvā adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaissati, tassa vinicchayau appaṭivattiyau buddhaina sayaṃ nisīditvā vinicchitasadisau hauti| taṃ cai aivaṃ vinicchayakusalaṃ bhikkhuṃ kauci katasikkhāpadavītik‍kamau  bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā attanau kuk‍kuc‍caṃ pucchaiyya, taina sādhukaṃ sal‍lakkhaitvā sacai anāpatti hauti, ‘‘anāpattī’’ti vattabbaṃ| sacai pana āpatti hauti, sā daisanāgāminī cai, ‘‘daisanāgāminī’’ti vattabbaṃ| vuṭṭhānagāminī cai, ‘‘vuṭṭhānagāminī’’ti vattabbaṃ| athassa pārājikacchāyā dissati, ‘‘pārājikāpattī’’ti na vattabbaṃ| kasmā? maithunadhammavītik‍kamau hi uttarimanussadhammavītik‍kamau ca auḷārikau, adin‍nādānamanaussaviggahavītik‍kamā pana sukhumā cittalahukā| tai sukhumainaiva āpaj‍jati, sukhumaina rakkhati, tasmā visaisaina taṃvatthukaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ pucchiyamānau ‘‘āpattī’’ti avatvā sacassa ācariyau dharati, tatau taina sau bhikkhu ‘‘amhākaṃ ācariyaṃ pucchā’’ti paisaitabbau| sacai sau  puna āgantvā ‘‘tumhākaṃ ācariyau suttatau nayatau aulaukaitvā ‘sataikicchau’ti maṃ āhā’’ti vadati, tatau taina sau ‘‘sādhu suṭṭhu yaṃ ācariyau bhaṇati, taṃ karauhī’’ti vattabbau| atha panassa ācariyau natthi, saddhiṃ uggahitatthairau pana atthi, tassa santikaṃ paisaitabbau ‘‘amhaihi saha uggahitatthairau gaṇapāmaukkhau, taṃ gantvā pucchā’’ti| tainapi ‘‘sataikicchau’’ti vinicchitai ‘‘sādhu suṭṭhu tassa vacanaṃ karauhī’’ti vattabbau| atha tassa saddhiṃ uggahitatthairaupi natthi, antaivāsikau paṇḍitau atthi, tassa santikaṃ paisaitabbau ‘‘asukadaharaṃ gantvā pucchā’’ti| tainapi ‘‘sataikicchau’’ti vinicchitai ‘‘sādhu suṭṭhu tassa vacanaṃ karauhī’’ti vattabbau| atha daharassapi pārājikacchāyāva upaṭṭhāti, tainapi ‘‘pārājikausī’’ti na vattabbau| dul‍labhau hi buddhuppādau, tatau dul‍labhatarā pabbaj‍jā ca upasampadā ca| aivaṃ pana vattabbau ‘‘vivittaṃ aukāsaṃ sammaj‍jitvā divāvihāraṃ nisīditvā sīlāni visaudhaitvā dvattiṃsākāraṃ tāva manasikarauhī’’ti| sacai tassa araugaṃ sīlaṃ, kammaṭṭhānaṃ ghaṭayati, saṅkhārā pākaṭā hutvā upaṭṭhahanti, upacārappanāppattaṃ viya cittaṃ aikaggaṃ hauti, divasaṃ atik‍kantampi na jānāti, sau divasātik‍kamai upaṭṭhānaṃ āgatau aivaṃ vattabbau ‘‘kīdisā tai cittappavattī’’ti| āraucitāya ca cittapavattiyā vattabbau ‘‘pabbaj‍jā nāma cittavisuddhatthāya, appamattau samaṇadhammaṃ karauhī’’ti|

yassa pana sīlaṃ bhin‍naṃ hauti, tassa kammaṭṭhānaṃ na ghaṭayati, pataudābhitun‍naṃ viya cittaṃ vikampati, vippaṭisāragginā ḍayhati, tattapāsāṇai nisin‍nau viya taṅkhaṇaiyaiva vuṭṭhāti| sau āgatau ‘‘kā tai cittapavattī’’ti pucchitabbau| āraucitāya cittapavattiyā ‘‘natthi laukai rahau nāma pāpakammaṃ pakubbatau| sabbapaṭhamañhi pāpaṃ karauntau attanā jānāti| athassa ārakkhadaivatā paracittavidū samaṇabrāhmaṇā añ‍ñā ca daivatā jānanti, tvaṃyaiva dāni tava sautthiṃ  pariyaisāhī’’ti vattabbau| aivaṃ katavītik‍kamainaiva bhikkhunā sayamaiva āgantvā āraucitai paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ|

235. idāni yā sā pubbai vuttappabhaidā caudanā, tassāyaiva sampattivipattijānanatthaṃ ādimajjhapariyausānādīnaṃ vasaina vinicchayau vaiditabbau| saiyyathidaṃ, caudanāya kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? caudanāya ‘‘ahaṃ taṃ vattukāmau, karautu mai āyasmā aukāsa’’nti aivaṃ aukāsakammaṃ ādi| autiṇṇaina vatthunā caudaitvā sāraitvā vinicchayau majjhai| āpattiyaṃ vā anāpattiyaṃ vā patiṭṭhāpanaina samathau pariyausānaṃ|

caudanāya kati mūlāni, kati vatthūni, kati bhūmiyau? caudanāya dvai mūlāni samūlikā vā amūlikā vā| tīṇi vatthūni diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ parisaṅkitaṃ| pañ‍ca bhūmiyau kālaina vakkhāmi, nau akālaina, bhūtaina vakkhāmi, nau abhūtaina, saṇhaina vakkhāmi, nau pharusaina, atthasaṃhitaina vakkhāmi, nau anatthasaṃhitaina, maittacittau vakkhāmi, nau dausantarauti| imāya ca pana caudanāya caudakaina puggalaina ‘‘parisuddhakāyasamācārau nu khaumhī’’tiādinā (pari॰ 436) nayaina upālipañ‍cakaisu vuttaisu pan‍narasasu dhammaisu patiṭṭhātabbaṃ| cuditakaina dvīsu dhammaisu patiṭṭhātabbaṃ sac‍cai ca akuppai cāti|

anuvij‍jakaina (pari॰ 360) ca caudakau pucchitabbau ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisi, kimhi naṃ caudaisi, sīlavipattiyā caudaisi, ācāravipattiyā caudaisi, diṭṭhivipattiyā caudaisī’’ti| sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘sīlavipattiyā vā caudaimi, ācāravipattiyā vā caudaimi, diṭṭhivipattiyā vā caudaimī’’ti| sau aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘jānāsi panāyasmā sīlavipattiṃ, jānāsi ācāravipattiṃ, jānāsi diṭṭhivipatti’’nti| sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘jānāmi khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sīlavipattiṃ, jānāmi ācāravipattiṃ, jānāmi diṭṭhivipatti’’nti| sau aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘katamā panāvusau, sīlavipatti, katamā ācāravipatti, katamā diṭṭhivipattī’’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘cattāri pārājikāni tairasa saṅghādisaisā, ayaṃ sīlavipatti| thul‍lac‍cayaṃ pācittiyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ dubbhāsitaṃ, ayaṃ ācāravipatti| micchādiṭṭhi antaggāhikā diṭṭhi, ayaṃ diṭṭhivipattī’’ti|

sau aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisi, diṭṭhaina vā caudaisi, sutaina vā caudaisi, parisaṅkāya vā caudaisī’’ti| sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘diṭṭhaina vā  caudaimi, sutaina vā caudaimi, parisaṅkāya vā caudaimī’’ti| sau aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ diṭṭhaina caudaisi, kiṃ tai diṭṭhaṃ, kinti tai diṭṭhaṃ, kadā tai diṭṭhaṃ, kattha tai diṭṭhaṃ, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau, saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau, thul‍lac‍cayaṃ pācittiyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau, kattha cāyaṃ bhikkhu ahausi, kattha ca tvaṃ karausi, kiñ‍ca tvaṃ karausi, kiṃ ayaṃ bhikkhu karautī’’ti?

sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ diṭṭhaina caudaimi, apica sutaina caudaimī’’ti| sau aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ sutaina caudaisi, kiṃ tai sutaṃ, kinti tai sutaṃ, kadā tai sutaṃ, kattha tai sutaṃ, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti sutaṃ, saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti sutaṃ, thul‍lac‍cayaṃ pācittiyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti sutaṃ, bhikkhussa sutaṃ, bhikkhuniyā sutaṃ, sikkhamānāya sutaṃ, sāmaṇairassa sutaṃ, sāmaṇairiyā sutaṃ, upāsakassa sutaṃ, upāsikāya sutaṃ, rājūnaṃ sutaṃ, rājamahāmattānaṃ sutaṃ, titthiyānaṃ sutaṃ, titthiyasāvakānaṃ suta’’nti|

sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ sutaina caudaimi, apica parisaṅkāya caudaimī’’ti| sau aivamassa vacanīyau ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ parisaṅkāya caudaisi, kiṃ parisaṅkasi, kinti parisaṅkasi, kadā parisaṅkasi, kattha parisaṅkasi? pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti parisaṅkasi, saṅghādisaisaṃ thul‍lac‍cayaṃ pācittiyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti parisaṅkasi, bhikkhussa sutvā parisaṅkasi, bhikkhuniyā sutvā…pai॰… titthiyasāvakānaṃ sutvā parisaṅkasī’’ti|

diṭṭhaṃ diṭṭhaina samaiti, diṭṭhaina saṃsandatai diṭṭhaṃ|

diṭṭhaṃ paṭic‍ca na upaiti, asuddhaparisaṅkitau|

sau puggalau paṭiñ‍ñāya, kātabbau tainupausathau||

sutaṃ sutaina samaiti, sutaina saṃsandatai sutaṃ|

sutaṃ paṭic‍ca na upaiti, asuddhaparisaṅkitau|

sau puggalau paṭiñ‍ñāya, kātabbau tainupausathau||

mutaṃ  mutaina samaiti, mutaina saṃsandatai mutaṃ|

mutaṃ paṭic‍ca na upaiti, asuddhaparisaṅkitau|

sau puggalau paṭiñ‍ñāya, kātabbau tainupausathau||

paṭiñ‍ñā laj‍jīsu katā, alaj‍jīsu aivaṃ na vij‍jati|

bahumpi alaj‍jī bhāsaiyya, vattānusandhitaina kārayaiti|| (pari॰ 359)|

apicaittha saṅgāmāvacaraina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamantaina nīcacittaina saṅghau upasaṅkamitabbau rajauharaṇasamaina cittaina, āsanakusalaina bhavitabbaṃ nisaj‍jakusalaina, thairai bhikkhū anupakhaj‍jantaina navai bhikkhū āsanaina appaṭibāhantaina yathāpatirūpai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, anānākathikaina bhavitabbaṃ atiracchānakathikaina, sāmaṃ vā dhammau bhāsitabbau, parau vā ajjhaisitabbau, ariyau vā tuṇhībhāvau nātimañ‍ñitabbau|

saṅghaina anumataina puggalaina anuvij‍jakaina anuvij‍jitukāmaina na upajjhāyau pucchitabbau, na ācariyau pucchitabbau, na saddhivihārikau pucchitabbau, na antaivāsikau pucchitabbau, na samānupajjhāyakau pucchitabbau, na samānācariyakau pucchitabbau, na jāti pucchitabbā, na nāmaṃ pucchitabbaṃ, na gauttaṃ pucchitabbaṃ, na āgamau pucchitabbau, na kulapadaisau pucchitabbau, na jātibhūmi pucchitabbā| taṃ kiṃkāraṇā? atrassa paimaṃ vā dausau vā, paimai vā sati dausai vā chandāpi gacchaiyya dausāpi gacchaiyya mauhāpi gacchaiyya bhayāpi gacchaiyyāti|

saṅghaina anumataina puggalaina anuvij‍jakaina anuvij‍jitukāmaina saṅghagarukaina bhavitabbaṃ, nau puggalagarukaina, saddhammagarukaina bhavitabbaṃ, nau āmisagarukaina, atthavasikaina bhavitabbaṃ, nau parisakappikaina, kālaina anuvij‍jitabbaṃ, nau akālaina, bhūtaina anuvij‍jitabbaṃ, nau abhūtaina, saṇhaina anuvij‍jitabbaṃ, nau pharusaina, atthasaṃhitaina anuvij‍jitabbaṃ, nau anatthasaṃhitaina, maittacittaina anuvij‍jitabbaṃ, nau dausantaraina, na upakaṇṇakajappinā bhavitabbaṃ, na jimhaṃ paikkhitabbaṃ, na akkhi nikhaṇitabbaṃ, na bhamukaṃ ukkhipitabbaṃ, na sīsaṃ ukkhipitabbaṃ, na hatthavikārau kātabbau, na hatthamuddā dassaitabbā|

āsanakusalaina  bhavitabbaṃ nisaj‍jakusalaina, yugamattaṃ paikkhantaina atthaṃ anuvidhiyantaina sakai  āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na ca āsanā vuṭṭhātabbaṃ, na vītihātabbaṃ, na kummaggau saivitabbau, na bāhāvikkhaipakaṃ bhaṇitabbaṃ, aturitaina bhavitabbaṃ asāhasikaina, acaṇḍikataina bhavitabbaṃ vacanakkhamaina, maittacittaina bhavitabbaṃ hitānukampinā, kāruṇikaina bhavitabbaṃ hitaparisak‍kinā, asamphappalāpinā bhavitabbaṃ pariyantabhāṇinā, avairavasikaina bhavitabbaṃ anasuruttaina, attā pariggahaitabbau, parau pariggahaitabbau, caudakau pariggahaitabbau, cuditakau pariggahaitabbau, adhammacaudakau pariggahaitabbau, adhammacuditakau pariggahaitabbau, dhammacaudakau pariggahaitabbau, dhammacuditakau pariggahaitabbau, vuttaṃ ahāpaintaina avuttaṃ appakāsaintaina autiṇṇāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā parau paripucchitvā yathāpaṭiñ‍ñāya kāraitabbau, mandau hāsaitabbau, bhīru assāsaitabbau, caṇḍau nisaidhaitabbau, asuci vibhāvaitabbau, ujumaddavaina na chandāgati gantabbā, na dausāgati gantabbā, na mauhāgati gantabbā, na bhayāgati gantabbā, majjhattaina bhavitabbaṃ dhammaisu ca puggalaisu ca, aivañ‍ca pana anuvij‍jakau anuvij‍jamānau satthu caiva sāsanakarau hauti, viñ‍ñūnañ‍ca sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cāti|

iti pāḷimuttakavinayavinicchayasaṅgahai

caudanādivinicchayakathā samattā|

32. garukāpattivuṭṭhānavinicchayakathā

236. garukāpattivuṭṭhānanti  parivāsamānattādīhi vinayakammaihi garukāpattitau vuṭṭhānaṃ| tattha (cūḷava॰ aṭṭha॰ 102) tividhau parivāsau paṭicchan‍naparivāsau suddhantaparivāsau samaudhānaparivāsauti| taisu paṭicchan‍naparivāsau tāva yathāpaṭicchan‍nāya āpattiyā dātabbau| kassaci hi aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā āpatti hauti, kassaci dvīhappaṭicchan‍nā, kassaci aikāpatti hauti, kassaci dvai tissau tatuttari vā| tasmā paṭicchan‍naparivāsaṃ daintaina  paṭhamaṃ tāva paṭicchan‍nabhāvau jānitabbau| ayañhi āpatti nāma dasahākāraihi paṭicchan‍nā hauti|

tatrāyaṃ mātikā – āpatti ca hauti āpattisañ‍ñī ca, pakatattau ca hauti pakatattasañ‍ñī ca, anantarāyikau ca hauti anantarāyikasañ‍ñī ca, pahu ca hauti pahusañ‍ñī ca, chādaitukāmau ca hauti chādaiti cāti| tattha āpatti ca hauti āpattisañ‍ñī cāti yaṃ āpan‍nau, sā āpattiyaiva hauti, saupi ca tattha āpattisañ‍ñīyaiva| iti jānantau chādaiti, chan‍nā hauti, atha panāyaṃ tattha anāpattisañ‍ñī, acchan‍nā hauti| anāpatti pana āpattisañ‍ñāyapi anāpattisañ‍ñāyapi chādaintaina acchāditāva hauti, lahukaṃ vā garukāti garukaṃ vā lahukāti chādaiti, alaj‍jipakkhai tiṭṭhati, āpatti pana acchan‍nā hauti, garukaṃ lahukāti mañ‍ñamānau daisaiti, naiva daisitā hauti, na chan‍nā, garukaṃ vā garukāti ñatvā chādaiti, chan‍nā hauti, garukalahukabhāvaṃ na jānāti, āpattiṃ chādaimīti chādaiti, chan‍nāva hauti|

pakatattauti tividhaṃ ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ akatau| sau cai pakatattasañ‍ñī hutvā chādaiti, chan‍nā hauti| atha ‘‘mayhaṃ saṅghaina kammaṃ kata’’nti apakatattasañ‍ñī hutvā chādaiti, acchan‍nā hauti| apakatattaina pana pakatattasañ‍ñinā vā apakatattasañ‍ñinā vā chāditāpi acchan‍nāva hauti| vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘āpaj‍jati garukaṃ sāvasaisaṃ,

chādaiti anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca|

na  bhikkhunī nau ca phusaiyya vaj‍jaṃ,

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā’’ti|| (pari॰ 481) –

ayañhi pañhau ukkhittakaina kathitau|

anantarāyikauti yassa dasasu antarāyaisu aikaupi natthi, sau cai anantarāyikasañ‍ñī hutvā chādaiti, chan‍nā hauti| sacaipi sau bhīrujātikatāya andhakārai amanussacaṇḍamigabhayaina antarāyikasañ‍ñī hutvā chādaiti, acchan‍nāva hauti| yassapi pabbatavihārai vasantassa kandaraṃ  vā nadiṃ vā atik‍kamitvā āraucaitabbaṃ hauti, antarāmaggai ca caṇḍavāḷaamanussādibhayaṃ atthi, maggai ajagarā nipaj‍janti, nadī pūrā hauti, aitasmiṃ pana satiyaiva antarāyai antarāyikasañ‍ñī chādaiti, acchan‍nā hauti| antarāyikassa pana antarāyikasañ‍ñāya chādayatau acchan‍nāva|

pahūti sau sak‍kauti bhikkhunau santikaṃ gantuñ‍caiva āraucaituñ‍ca, sau cai pahusañ‍ñī hutvā chādaiti, chan‍nā hauti| sacassa mukhai appamattakau gaṇḍau vā hauti, hanukavātau vā vijjhati, dantau vā ruj‍jati, bhikkhā vā mandā laddhā hauti, tāvatakaina pana naiva vattuṃ na sak‍kauti, na gantuṃ, apica khau ‘‘na sak‍kaumī’’ti sañ‍ñī hauti, ayaṃ pahu hutvā appahusañ‍ñī nāma| iminā chāditāpi acchāditā| appahunā pana vattuṃ vā gantuṃ vā asamatthaina pahusañ‍ñinā vā appahusañ‍ñinā vā chāditā hauti, acchāditāva|

chādaitukāmau ca hauti chādaiti cāti idaṃ uttānatthamaiva| sacai pana ‘‘chādaissāmī’’ti dhuranikkhaipaṃ katvā puraibhattai vā pacchābhattai vā paṭhamayāmādīsu vā laj‍jidhammaṃ auk‍kamitvā antauaruṇaiyaiva āraucaiti, ayaṃ chādaitukāmau na chādaiti nāma| yassa pana abhikkhukai ṭhānai vasantassa āpaj‍jitvā sabhāgassa bhikkhunau āgamanaṃ āgamaintassa, sabhāgassa santikaṃ vā gacchantassa aḍḍhamāsaupi māsaupi atik‍kamati, ayaṃ na chādaitukāmau chādaiti nāma, ayampi acchan‍nāva hauti| yau pana āpan‍namattauva aggiṃ ak‍kantapurisau viya sahasā pak‍kamitvā sabhāgaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā āvikarauti, ayaṃ na chādaitukāmauva na chādaiti nāma| sacai pana sabhāgaṃ disvāpi ‘‘ayaṃ mai upajjhāyau vā ācariyau vā’’ti laj‍jāya nāraucaiti, chan‍nāva hauti āpatti| upajjhāyādibhāvau hi idha appamāṇaṃ, avairisabhāgamattamaiva pamāṇaṃ| tasmā avairisabhāgassa  santikai āraucaitabbā| yau pana visabhāgau hauti sutvā pakāsaitukāmau, aivarūpassa upajjhāyassapi santikai na āraucaitabbā|

tattha puraibhattaṃ vā āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hautu pacchābhattaṃ vā divā vā rattiṃ vā, yāva aruṇaṃ na uggacchati, tāva āraucaitabbaṃ| uddhastai  aruṇai paṭicchan‍nā hauti, paṭicchādanapac‍cayā ca duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍jati, sabhāgasaṅghādisaisaṃ āpan‍nassa pana santikai āvikātuṃ na vaṭṭati| sacai āvikarauti, āpatti āvikatā hauti, duk‍kaṭā pana na muc‍cati| tasmā suddhassa santikai āvikātabbā| āvikarauntau ca ‘‘tuyhaṃ santikai aikaṃ āpattiṃ āvikaraumī’’ti vā ‘‘ācikkhāmī’’ti vā āraucaimī’’ti vā ‘‘mama aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nabhāvaṃ jānāhī’’ti vā vadatu, ‘‘aikaṃ garukāpattiṃ āvikaraumī’’tiādinā vā nayaina vadatu, sabbaihipi ākāraihi appaṭicchan‍nāva hautīti kurundiyaṃ vuttaṃ| sacai pana ‘‘lahukāpattiṃ āvikaraumī’’tiādinā nayaina vadati, paṭicchan‍nāva hauti| vatthuṃ āraucaiti, āpattiṃ āraucaiti, ubhayaṃ āraucaiti, tividhainapi āraucitāva hauti|

237. iti imāni dasa kāraṇāni upaparikkhitvā paṭicchan‍naparivāsaṃ daintaina paṭhamamaiva paṭicchan‍nabhāvau jānitabbau, tatau paṭicchan‍nadivasai ca āpattiyau ca sal‍lakkhaitvā sacai aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā hauti, ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍na’’nti aivaṃ yācāpaitvā khandhakai (cūḷava॰ 98) āgatanayainaiva kammavācaṃ vatvā parivāsau dātabbau| atha dvīhatīhādipaṭicchan‍nā hauti, ‘‘dvīhappaṭicchan‍naṃ, tīhappaṭicchan‍naṃ, catūhappaṭicchan‍naṃ, pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… cuddasāhappaṭicchan‍na’’nti aivaṃ yāva cuddasadivasāni divasavasaina yaujanā kātabbā, pañ‍cadasadivasapaṭicchan‍nāya ‘‘pakkhapaṭicchan‍na’’nti yaujanā kātabbā| tatau yāva aikūnatiṃsatimau divasau, tāva ‘‘atiraikapakkhapaṭicchan‍na’’nti, tatau ‘‘māsapaṭicchan‍naṃ, atiraikamāsapaṭicchan‍naṃ, dvaimāsapaṭicchan‍naṃ, atiraikadvaimāsapaṭicchan‍naṃ, taimāsa…pai॰… atiraikaaikādasamāsapaṭicchan‍na’’nti aivaṃ yaujanā kātabbā| saṃvaccharai puṇṇai ‘‘aikasaṃvaccharapaṭicchan‍na’’nti, tatau paraṃ ‘‘atiraikasaṃvaccharaṃ, dvaisaṃvacchara’’nti aivaṃ yāva ‘‘saṭṭhisaṃvaccharaṃ, atiraikasaṭṭhisaṃvaccharapaṭicchan‍na’’nti vā tatau vā bhiyyaupi vatvā yaujanā kātabbā|

sacai pana dvai tissau tatuttari vā āpattiyau haunti, yathā ‘‘aikaṃ āpatti’’nti vuttaṃ, aivaṃ ‘‘dvai āpattiyau, tissau āpattiyau’’ti vattabbaṃ| tatau paraṃ pana sataṃ vā hautu sahassaṃ vā, ‘‘sambahulā’’ti vattuṃ vaṭṭati| nānāvatthukāsupi ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā  āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ, aikaṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ, aikaṃ duṭṭhul‍lavācaṃ, aikaṃ attakāmaṃ, aikaṃ sañ‍carittaṃ, aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau’’ti aivaṃ gaṇanavasaina vā ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ nānāvatthukā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau’’ti aivaṃ vatthukittanavasaina vā ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau’’ti aivaṃ nāmamattavasaina vā yaujanā kātabbā| tattha nāmaṃ duvidhaṃ sajātisādhāraṇaṃ sabbasādhāraṇañ‍ca| tattha saṅghādisaisauti sajātisādhāraṇaṃ| āpattīti sabbasādhāraṇaṃ| tasmā ‘‘sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau’’ti aivaṃ sabbasādhāraṇanāmavasainapi vaṭṭati| idañhi parivāsādivinayakammaṃ vatthuvasaina gauttavasaina nāmavasaina āpattivasaina ca kātuṃ vaṭṭatiyaiva|

tattha suk‍kavissaṭṭhīti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca| saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca| tattha ‘‘suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ kāyasaṃsagga’’ntiādinā vacanainapi ‘‘nānāvatthukāyau’’ti vacanainapi vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca gahitaṃ hauti| ‘‘saṅghādisaisau’’ti vacanainapi ‘‘āpattiyau’’ti vacanainapi nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca gahitā hauti| tasmā aitaisu yassa kassaci vasaina kammavācā kātabbā| idha pana sabbāpattīnaṃ sādhāraṇavasaina sambahulanayainaiva ca sabbattha kammavācaṃ yaujaitvā dassayissāma| aikañhi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā ‘‘sambahulā’’ti vinayakammaṃ karauntassapi vuṭṭhāti aikaṃ vinā sambahulānaṃ abhāvatau| sambahulā pana āpaj‍jitvā ‘‘aikaṃ āpaj‍ji’’nti karauntassa na vuṭṭhāti, tasmā sambahulanayainaiva yaujayissāma| saiyyathidaṃ – paṭicchan‍naparivāsaṃ daintaina sacai aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā āpatti hauti|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, dutiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tatiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yācāmīti –

aivaṃ tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsau, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ yau yau āpan‍nau hauti, tassa tassa nāmaṃ gahaitvā kammavācā kātabbā|

kammavācāpariyausānai ca taina bhikkhunā māḷakasīmāyamaiva ‘‘parivāsaṃ samādiyāmi, vattaṃ samādiyāmī’’ti vattaṃ samādātabbaṃ, samādiyitvā tatthaiva saṅghassa āraucaitabbaṃ| āraucaintaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ  aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ, bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

aivaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ| imañ‍ca atthaṃ gahaitvā yāya kāyaci vācāya āraucaituṃ vaṭṭatiyaiva|

āraucaitvā  (cūḷava॰ aṭṭha॰ 102) sacai nikkhipitukāmau hauti, ‘‘parivāsaṃ nikkhipāmi, vattaṃ nikkhipāmī’’ti nikkhipitabbaṃ| aikapadainapi caittha nikkhittau hauti parivāsau, dvīhi pana sunikkhittauyaiva| samādānaipi aisaiva nayau| nikkhittakālatau paṭṭhāya pakatattaṭṭhānai tiṭṭhati| māḷakatau bhikkhūsu nikkhantaisu aikassapi santikai nikkhipituṃ vaṭṭati, māḷakatau nikkhamitvā satiṃ paṭilabhantaina sahagacchantassa santikai nikkhipitabbaṃ| sacai saupi pak‍kantau, añ‍ñassa yassa māḷakai nāraucitaṃ, tassa āraucaitvā nikkhipitabbaṃ| āraucaintaina ca avasānai ‘‘vaidiyatīti maṃ āyasmā dhāraitū’’ti vattabbaṃ| dvin‍naṃ āraucaintaina ‘‘āyasmantā dhāraintū’’ti, tiṇṇaṃ āraucaintaina ‘‘āyasmantau dhāraintū’’ti vattabbaṃ| sacai appabhikkhukau vihārau hauti, sabhāgā bhikkhū vasanti, vattaṃ anikkhipitvā vihāraiyaiva rattipariggahau kātabbau| atha na sak‍kā saudhaituṃ, vuttanayainaiva vattaṃ nikkhipitvā pac‍cūsasamayai aikaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ parikkhittassa vihārassa parikkhaipatau, aparikkhittassa vihārassa parikkhaipārahaṭṭhānatau dvai laiḍḍupātai atik‍kamitvā mahāmaggatau auk‍kamma gumbaina vā vatiyā vā paṭicchan‍naṭṭhānai nisīditabbaṃ, antauaruṇaiyaiva vuttanayaina vattaṃ samādiyitvā āraucaitabbaṃ| āraucaintaina sacai navakatarau hauti, ‘‘āvusau’’ti vattabbaṃ| sacai vuḍḍhatarau, ‘‘bhantai’’ti vattabbaṃ| sacai añ‍ñau kauci bhikkhu kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina taṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati, sacai aisa taṃ passati, saddaṃ vāssa suṇāti, āraucaitabbaṃ, anāraucaintassa ratticchaidau caiva vattabhaidau ca| atha dvādasahatthaṃ upacāraṃ auk‍kamitvā ajānantassaiva gacchati, ratticchaidau hautiyaiva, vattabhaidau pana natthi, uggatai aruṇai vattaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ| sacai sau bhikkhu kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina pak‍kantau hauti, yaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sabbapaṭhamaṃ passati, tassa āraucaitvā nikkhipitabbaṃ| vihāraṃ gantvāpi yaṃ paṭhamaṃ passati, tassa āraucaitvā nikkhipitabbaṃ| ayaṃ nikkhittavattassa parihārau|

238. aivaṃ  yattakāni divasāni āpatti paṭicchan‍nā hauti, tattakāni tatau adhikatarāni vā kuk‍kuc‍cavinaudanatthāya parivasitvā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā vattaṃ samādiyitvā mānattaṃ yācitabbaṃ| ayañhi vattai samādin‍nai aiva mānattārahau hauti nikkhittavattaina parivutthattā| anikkhittavattassa pana puna samādānakic‍caṃ natthi| sau hi paṭicchan‍nadivasātik‍kamainaiva mānattārahau hauti, tasmā tassa mānattaṃ dātabbamaiva| taṃ daintaina –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ  āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau, dutiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau, tatiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ  sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ  kammavācā kātabbā|

kammavācāpariyausānai ca taina bhikkhunā māḷakasīmāyamaiva ‘‘mānattaṃ samādiyāmi, vattaṃ samādiyāmī’’ti vattaṃ samādātabbaṃ, samādiyitvā tatthaiva saṅghassa āraucaitabbaṃ| āraucaintaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

aivaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ| imañ‍ca pana atthaṃ gahaitvā yāya kāyaci vācāya āraucaituṃ vaṭṭatiyaiva|

āraucaitvā  sacai nikkhipitukāmau hauti, ‘‘mānattaṃ nikkhipāmi, vattaṃ nikkhipāmī’’ti saṅghamajjhai nikkhipitabbaṃ| māḷakatau bhikkhūsu nikkhantaisu aikassapi santikai nikkhipituṃ vaṭṭati| māḷakatau nikkhamitvā satiṃ paṭilabhantaina sahagacchantassa santikai nikkhipitabbaṃ| sacai saupi pak‍kantau, añ‍ñassa yassa māḷakai nāraucitaṃ, tassa āraucaitvā nikkhipitabbaṃ| āraucaintaina pana avasānai ‘‘vaidiyatīti maṃ āyasmā dhāraitū’’ti vattabbaṃ| dvin‍naṃ āraucaintaina ‘‘āyasmantā dhāraintū’’ti, tiṇṇaṃ āraucaintaina ‘‘āyasmantau dhāraintū’’ti vattabbaṃ| nikkhittakālatau paṭṭhāya pakatattaṭṭhānai tiṭṭhati| sacai appabhikkhukau vihārau hauti, sabhāgā bhikkhū vasanti, vattaṃ anikkhipitvā antauvihāraiyaiva rattiyau gaṇaitabbā| atha na sak‍kā saudhaituṃ, vuttanayainaiva vattaṃ nikkhipitvā pac‍cūsasamayai catūhi pañ‍cahi vā bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ parikkhittassa vihārassa parikkhaipatau, aparikkhittassa parikkhaipārahaṭṭhānatau dvai laiḍḍupātai atik‍kamitvā mahāmaggatau auk‍kamma gumbaina vā vatiyā vā paṭicchan‍naṭṭhānai nisīditabbaṃ, antauaruṇaiyaiva vuttanayaina vattaṃ samādiyitvā āraucaitabbaṃ| sacai añ‍ñau kauci bhikkhu kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina taṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati, sacai aisa taṃ passati, saddaṃ vāssa suṇāti, āraucaitabbaṃ| anāraucaintassa ratticchaidau caiva vattabhaidau ca, atha dvādasahatthaṃ upacāraṃ auk‍kamitvā ajānantassaiva gacchati, ratticchaidau hauti aiva, vattabhaidau  pana natthi| āraucitakālatau paṭṭhāya aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ ṭhapaitvā saisaihi sati karaṇīyai gantumpi vaṭṭati, aruṇai uṭṭhitai tassa bhikkhussa santikai vattaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ| sacai saupi kainaci kammaina purai aruṇaiyaiva gacchati, añ‍ñaṃ vihāratau nikkhantaṃ vā āgantukaṃ vā yaṃ paṭhamaṃ passati, tassa santikai āraucaitvā vattaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ| ayañ‍ca yasmā gaṇassa āraucaitvā bhikkhūnañ‍ca atthibhāvaṃ sal‍lakkhaitvāva vasi, tainassa ūnai gaṇai caraṇadausau vā vippavāsau vā na hauti| sacai na kañ‍ci passati, vihāraṃ gantvāpi yaṃ paṭhamaṃ passati, tassa āraucaitvā nikkhipitabbaṃ| ayaṃ nikkhittavattassa parihārau|

239. aivaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ akhaṇḍaṃ caritvā yattha siyā vīsatigaṇau bhikkhusaṅghau, tattha sau bhikkhu abbhaitabbau| abbhaintaihi ca paṭhamaṃ  abbhānārahau kātabbau| ayañhi nikkhittavattattā pakatattaṭṭhānai ṭhitau, pakatattassa ca abbhānaṃ kātuṃ na vaṭṭati, tasmā vattaṃ samādāpaitabbau, vattai samādin‍nai abbhānārahau hauti| tainapi vattaṃ samādiyitvā āraucaitvā abbhānaṃ yācitabbaṃ| anikkhittavattassa puna vattasamādānakic‍caṃ natthi| sau hi chārattātik‍kamainaiva abbhānārahau hauti, tasmā sau abbhaitabbau| abbhaintaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau dutiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau tatiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ  aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ  sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘abbhitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

aivaṃ tāva aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya āpattiyā paṭicchan‍naparivāsau mānattadānaṃ abbhānañ‍ca vaiditabbaṃ| imināva nayaina dvīhādipaṭicchan‍nāsupi tadanurūpā kammavācā kātabbā|

240. sacai pana appaṭicchan‍nā āpatti hauti, parivāsaṃ adatvā mānattamaiva datvā ciṇṇamānattau abbhaitabbau| kathaṃ? mānattaṃ daintaina tāva –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… dutiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… tatiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ  āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

kammavācāpariyausānai ca vattasamādānaṃ vattanikkhaipau mānattacaraṇañ‍ca sabbaṃ vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ| āraucaintaina pana –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ  āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ, bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

aivaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ|

aikassa dvin‍naṃ tiṇṇaṃ vā āraucaintaina paṭicchan‍namānattai vuttanayainaiva āraucaitabbaṃ| ciṇṇamānattau ca yattha siyā vīsatigaṇau bhikkhusaṅghau, tattha sau abbhaitabbau| abbhaintaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau dutiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau tatiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

abbhitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācaṃ vatvā abbhaitabbau| aivaṃ appaṭicchan‍nāya āpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

241. sacai  kassaci aikāpatti paṭicchan‍nā hauti, aikā appaṭicchan‍nā, tassa paṭicchan‍nāya āpattiyā parivāsaṃ datvā parivutthaparivāsassa mānattaṃ daintaina appaṭicchan‍nāpattiṃ paṭicchan‍nāpattiyā samaudhānaitvāpi dātuṃ vaṭṭati| kathaṃ? sacai paṭicchan‍nāpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā hauti –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau, ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau  āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

kammavācāpariyausānai ca vattasamādānādi sabbaṃ vuttanayamaiva| āraucaintaina pana –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau, ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ, bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

aivaṃ  āraucaitabbaṃ|

samādin‍namānattaina ca anūnaṃ katvā vuttanayaina chārattaṃ mānattaṃ caritabbaṃ| ciṇṇamānattau ca yattha siyā vīsatigaṇau bhikkhusaṅghau, tattha sau abbhaitabbau| abbhaintaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau, ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nāñ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau  bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ  mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘abbhitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācaṃ katvā abbhaitabbau|

paṭicchan‍naparivāsakathā niṭṭhitā|

242. suddhantaparivāsau samaudhānaparivāsauti dvai avasaisā| tattha (cūḷava॰ aṭṭha॰ 102) suddhantaparivāsau duvidhau cūḷasuddhantau mahāsuddhantauti| duvidhaupi caisa rattiparicchaidaṃ sakalaṃ vā aikac‍caṃ vā ajānantassa ca assarantassa ca tattha vaimatikassa ca dātabbau| āpattipariyantaṃ pana ‘‘aittakā ahaṃ āpattiyau āpan‍nau’’ti jānātu vā mā vā, akāraṇamaitaṃ, tattha yau upasampadatau paṭṭhāya anulaumak‍kamaina vā āraucitadivasatau paṭṭhāya paṭilaumak‍kamaina vā ‘‘asukañ‍ca asukañ‍ca divasaṃ vā pakkhaṃ vā māsaṃ vā saṃvaccharaṃ vā tava suddhabhāvaṃ jānāsī’’ti pucchiyamānau ‘‘āma, bhantai, jānāmi, aittakaṃ nāma kālaṃ ahaṃ suddhau’’ti vadati, tassa din‍nau suddhantaparivāsau cūḷasuddhantauti vuc‍cati|

taṃ gahaitvā parivasantaina yattakaṃ kālaṃ attanau suddhiṃ jānāti, tattakaṃ apanaitvā avasaisaṃ māsaṃ vā dvaimāsaṃ vā parivasitabbaṃ| sacai ‘‘māsamattaṃ asuddhaumhī’’ti sal‍lakkhaitvā aggahaisi, parivasantau ca puna añ‍ñaṃ māsaṃ sarati, tampi māsaṃ parivasitabbamaiva, puna parivāsadānakic‍caṃ natthi| atha ‘‘dvaimāsaṃ asuddhaumhī’’ti sal‍lakkhaitvā aggahaisi, parivasantau ca ‘‘māsamattamaivāhaṃ asuddhaumhī’’ti san‍niṭṭhānaṃ karauti, māsamaiva parivasitabbaṃ, puna parivāsadānakic‍caṃ  natthi| ayañhi suddhantaparivāsau nāma uddhampi  ārauhati, haiṭṭhāpi aurauhati| idamassa lakkhaṇaṃ| añ‍ñasmiṃ pana āpattivuṭṭhānai idaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ – yau appaṭicchan‍naṃ āpattiṃ ‘‘paṭicchan‍nā’’ti vinayakammaṃ karauti, tassāpatti vuṭṭhāti| yau paṭicchan‍naṃ ‘‘appaṭicchan‍nā’’ti vinayakammaṃ karauti, tassa na vuṭṭhāti| acirapaṭicchan‍naṃ ‘‘cirapaṭicchan‍nā’’ti karauntassapi vuṭṭhāti, cirapaṭicchan‍naṃ ‘‘acirapaṭicchan‍nā’’ti karauntassa na vuṭṭhāti| aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā ‘‘sambahulā’’ti karauntassa vuṭṭhāti aikaṃ vinā sambahulānaṃ abhāvatau| sambahulā pana āpaj‍jitvā ‘‘aikaṃ āpaj‍ji’’nti karauntassa na vuṭṭhāti|

yau pana yathāvuttaina anulaumapaṭilaumanayaina pucchiyamānaupi rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti nassarati, vaimatikau vā hauti, tassa din‍nau suddhantaparivāsau mahāsuddhantauti vuc‍cati| taṃ gahaitvā gahitadivasatau paṭṭhāya yāva upasampadadivasau, tāva rattiyau gaṇaitvā parivasitabbaṃ, ayaṃ uddhaṃ nārauhati, haiṭṭhā pana aurauhati| tasmā sacai parivasantau rattiparicchaidai san‍niṭṭhānaṃ karauti ‘‘māsau vā saṃvaccharau vā mayhaṃ āpan‍nassā’’ti, māsaṃ vā saṃvaccharaṃ vā parivasitabbaṃ|

parivāsayācanadānalakkhaṇaṃ panaittha aivaṃ vaiditabbaṃ – taina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, rattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’’ti|

dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau|

byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau  āpaj‍ji, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ  yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ| suddhantaparivāsau, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ suddhantaparivāsau dātabbau|

kammavācāpariyausānai vattasamādānādi sabbaṃ vuttanayamaiva| āraucaintaina pana –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, rattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ, bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti āraucaitabbaṃ|

aikassa  dvin‍naṃ vā tiṇṇaṃ vā āraucanaṃ vuttanayamaiva| parivutthaparivāsassa mānattaṃ daintaina –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, rattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mārattaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ  saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

kammavācāpariyausānai mānattasamādānādi sabbaṃ vuttanayamaiva| āraucaintaina pana –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, rattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ, bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

aivaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ|

ciṇṇamānattau ca yattha siyā vīsatigaṇau bhikkhusaṅghau, tattha sau bhikkhu abbhaitabbau| abbhaintaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, rattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ bhantai ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ  tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji, āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘abbhitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācaṃ katvā abbhaitabbau|

suddhantaparivāsakathā niṭṭhitā|

243. samaudhānaparivāsau  pana tividhau hauti – audhānasamaudhānau agghasamaudhānau missakasamaudhānauti| tattha (cūḷava॰ aṭṭha॰ 102) audhānasamaudhānau nāma antarāpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā  paṭicchādaintassa parivutthadivasai audhunitvā makkhaitvā purimāya āpattiyā mūladivasaparicchaidai pacchā āpan‍naṃ āpattiṃ samaudahitvā dātabbaparivāsau vuc‍cati|

ayaṃ panaittha vinicchayau – yau paṭicchan‍nāya āpattiyā parivāsaṃ gahaitvā parivasantau vā mānattārahau vā mānattaṃ carantau vā abbhānārahau vā anikkhittavattau añ‍ñaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā purimāya āpattiyā samā vā ūnatarā vā rattiyau paṭicchādaiti, tassa mūlāyapaṭikassanaina tai parivutthadivasai ca mānattaciṇṇadivasai ca sabbai audhunitvā adivasai katvā pacchā āpan‍nāpattiṃ mūlaāpattiyaṃ samaudhāya parivāsau dātabbau| taina sacai mūlāpatti pakkhapaṭicchan‍nā, antarāpatti ūnakapakkhapaṭicchan‍nā, puna pakkhamaiva parivāsau parivasitabbau| athāpi antarāpatti pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāva, pakkhamaiva parivasitabbaṃ| aitainupāyaina yāva saṭṭhivassapaṭicchan‍nā mūlāpatti, tāva vinicchayau vaiditabbau| saṭṭhivassānipi parivasitvā mānattārahau hutvāpi hi aikadivasaṃ antarāpattiṃ paṭicchādaitvā puna saṭṭhivassāni parivāsārahau hauti| aivaṃ mānattacārikamānattārahakālaipi āpan‍nāya āpattiyā mūlāyapaṭikassanai katai mānattaciṇṇadivasāpi parivāsavutthadivasāpi sabbai makkhitāva haunti| sacai pana nikkhittavattau āpaj‍jati, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahau nāma na hauti| kasmā? yasmā na sau parivasantau āpan‍nau, pakatattaṭṭhānai ṭhitau āpan‍nau, tasmā tassā āpattiyā visuṃ mānattaṃ caritabbaṃ| sacai paṭicchan‍nā hauti, parivāsaupi vasitabbau|

‘‘sacai pana antarāpatti mūlāpattitau atiraikapaṭicchan‍nā hauti, tattha kiṃ kātabba’’nti vuttai mahāsumatthairau āha ‘‘ataikicchau ayaṃ puggalau, ataikicchau nāma āvikārāpaitvā vissaj‍jaitabbau’’ti| mahāpadumatthairau panāha ‘‘kasmā ataikicchau nāma, nanu ayaṃ samuc‍cayakkhandhakau nāma buddhānaṃ ṭhitakālasadisau, āpatti nāma paṭicchan‍nā vā hautu appaṭicchan‍nā vā samakaūnataraatiraikapaṭicchan‍nā vā, vinayadharassa kammavācaṃ yaujaituṃ samatthabhāvauyaivaittha pamāṇaṃ, tasmā yā atiraikapaṭicchan‍nā  hauti, taṃ mūlāpattiṃ katvā tattha itaraṃ samaudhāya parivāsau dātabbau’’ti| ayaṃ audhānasamaudhānau nāma|

taṃ daintaina paṭhamaṃ mūlāya paṭikassitvā pacchā parivāsau dātabbau| sacai kauci bhikkhu pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāya āpattiyā parivasantau antarā anikkhittavattauva puna pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, taina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācāmī’’ti|

dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau|

byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ  pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanā, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘paṭikassitau  saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanā, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanā kātabbā|

aivañ‍ca samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau| taina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’’ti|

dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau|

byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ  āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|
