‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsau, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

kammavācāpariyausānai ca vattasamādānādi sabbaṃ pubbai vuttanayamaiva| āraucaintaina pana –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā  sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ, bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

aivaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ|

parivutthaparivāsassa mānattaṃ daintaina –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau antarā  sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ  mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau saṅghaṃ antarā  sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

kammavācāpariyausānai ca mānattasamādānādi sabbaṃ vuttanayamaiva| āraucaintaina pana –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

aivaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ|

ciṇṇamānattaṃ abbhaintaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ  tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau saṅghaṃ  antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ purimāsu āpattīsu samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘abbhitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

sacai  mānattārahau vā mānattaṃ carantau vā abbhānārahau vā anikkhittavattau antarāpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā paṭicchādaiti, vuttanayainaiva purimāpattiyā antarāpattiyā ca divasaparicchaidaṃ sal‍lakkhaitvā tadanurūpāya kammavācāya mūlāya paṭikassitvā parivāsaṃ datvā parivutthaparivāsassa mānattaṃ datvā ciṇṇamānattau abbhaitabbau| sacai pana paṭicchan‍nāya āpattiyā parivasantau antarāpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na paṭicchādaiti, tassa mūlāyapaṭikassanāyaiva kātabbā, puna parivāsadānakic‍caṃ natthi| mūlāyapaṭikassanaina pana parivutthadivasānaṃ makkhitattā puna āditau paṭṭhāya parivasitabbaṃ| parivutthaparivāsassa ca mūlāpattiyā antarāpattiṃ samaudhānaitvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ, ciṇṇamānattau ca abbhaitabbau| kathaṃ? mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ karauntaina tāva sacai mūlāpatti pakkhapaṭicchan‍nā hauti,

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ antarāsambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ  mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanā, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘paṭikassitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassanā khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

aivaṃ mūlāya paṭikassitaina puna āditau paṭṭhāya parivasitabbaṃ| parivasantaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ paṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ  appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ parivasāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ, bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

āraucaitabbaṃ|

parivutthaparivāsassa  mānattaṃ daintaina –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, taṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji  pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā  āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, taṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

kammavācāpariyausānai mānattasamādānādi sabbaṃ vuttanayamaiva| āraucaintaina pana –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ  adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

aivaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ|

ciṇṇamānattaṃ abbhaintaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ apaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ bhantai ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, taṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji pakkhapaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ pakkhapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, taṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ paṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca appaṭicchan‍nānañ‍ca chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘abbhitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

imināva nayaina mānattārahamānattacārikaabbhānārahakālaisupi antarāpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā appaṭicchādaintassa mūlāyapaṭikassanamaiva katvā mūlāpattiyā antarāpattiṃ samaudhānaitvā mānattaṃ datvā ciṇṇamānattassa abbhānaṃ kātabbaṃ| aittha pana ‘‘sauhaṃ parivasantau’’ti āgataṭṭhānai ‘‘sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau’’ti vā ‘‘sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carantau’’ti vā ‘‘sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau abbhānārahau’’ti vā vattabbaṃ|

sacai  pana appaṭicchan‍nāya āpattiyā mānattaṃ carantau antarāpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na paṭicchādaiti, sau mūlāya paṭikassitvā antarāpattiyā puna mānattaṃ datvā ciṇṇamānattau abbhaitabbau| kathaṃ? mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ karauntaina tāva –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau mānattaṃ carantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau mānattaṃ carantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ  āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanā, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘paṭikassitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ  mūlāyapaṭikassanā khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

aivaṃ mūlāya paṭikassitvā mānattaṃ daintaina –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau mānattaṃ carantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, taṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ  mūlāya paṭikassi, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ  mānattaṃ adāsi, sau mānattaṃ carantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, taṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

kammavācāpariyausānai mānattasamādānādi sabbaṃ vuttanayamaiva| āraucaintaina pana –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ  carantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ, bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

aivaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ|

ciṇṇamānattaṃ  abbhaintaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ, taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau mānattaṃ carantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau  saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci, taṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau mānattaṃ carantau antarā sambahulā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji appaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ  yāci, taṃ saṅghau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi, sau saṅghaṃ antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau antarā sambahulānaṃ āpattīnaṃ appaṭicchan‍nānaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘abbhitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācaṃ katvā abbhaitabbau|

abbhānārahakālaipi antarāpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā appaṭicchādaintassa imināva nayaina mūlāyapaṭikassanā mānattadānaṃ abbhānañ‍ca vaiditabbaṃ| kaivalaṃ  panaittha ‘‘mānattaṃ carantau’’ti avatvā ‘‘ciṇṇamānattau abbhānārahau’’ti vattabbaṃ|

audhānasamaudhānaparivāsakathā niṭṭhitā|

244. agghasamaudhānau (cūḷava॰ aṭṭha॰ 102) nāma sambahulāsu āpattīsu yā aikā vā dvai vā tissau vā sambahulā vā āpattiyau sabbacirapaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhāya tāsaṃ rattiparicchaidavasaina avasaisānaṃ ūnatarapaṭicchan‍nānaṃ āpattīnaṃ parivāsau dīyati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati agghasamaudhānau| yassa pana sataṃ āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aparampi sataṃ āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāti aivaṃ dasakkhattuṃ katvā āpattisahassaṃ divasasataṃ paṭicchan‍naṃ hauti, taina kiṃ kātabbanti? sabbā samaudahitvā dasa divasai parivasitabbaṃ| aivaṃ aikainaiva dasāhaina divasasatampi parivasitabbamaiva hauti| vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘dasasataṃ rattisataṃ, āpattiyau chādayitvāna|

dasa rattiyau vasitvāna, muc‍caiyya pārivāsikau’’ti|| (pari॰ 477)|

ayaṃ  agghasamaudhānau nāma|

tassa āraucanadānalakkhaṇaṃ aivaṃ vaiditabbaṃ – sacai kassaci bhikkhunau aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā hauti, aikā āpatti dvīhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā tīhapaṭicchan‍nā, aikā catūhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā chāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā sattāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā aṭṭhāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā navāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā hauti, taina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ sambahulā āpattiyau aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau sambahulā āpattiyau dvīhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’’ti|

dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau|

byattaina  bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji sambahulā āpattiyau aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji sambahulā āpattiyau aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā  āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsau, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

kammavācāpariyausānai vattasamādānādi sabbaṃ vuttanayamaiva| āraucaintaina pana –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ sambahulā āpattiyau aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivasāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ, bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti –

aivaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ|

parivutthaparivāsassa mānattaṃ daintaina –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ sambahulā āpattiyau aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ  yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji sambahulā āpattiyau aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji sambahulā āpattiyau aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācā kātabbā|

kammavācāpariyausānai ca mānattasamādānādi sabbaṃ vuttanayamaiva| āraucaintaina pana –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ sambahulā āpattiyau aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa  mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carāmi, vaidiyāmahaṃ, bhantai, vaidiyatīti maṃ saṅghau dhāraitū’’ti āraucaitabbaṃ|

ciṇṇamānattau abbhaitabbau| abbhaintaina ca –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ sambahulā āpattiyau aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ, tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sauhaṃ, bhantai, ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji sambahulā āpattiyau aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji sambahulā āpattiyau  aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… sambahulā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi, sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi, sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti, yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa| yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘abbhitau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti –

aivaṃ kammavācaṃ katvā abbhaitabbau|

agghasamaudhānaparivāsakathā niṭṭhitā|

245. missakasamaudhānau (cuḷava॰ aṭṭha॰ 102) nāma – yau nānāvatthukā āpattiyau aikatau katvā dīyati| tatrāyaṃ nayau –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ aikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ, aikaṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ, aikaṃ duṭṭhul‍lavācaṃ, aikaṃ attakāmaṃ, aikaṃ sañ‍carittaṃ, aikaṃ kuṭikāraṃ, aikaṃ vihārakāraṃ, aikaṃ duṭṭhadausaṃ, aikaṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ, aikaṃ saṅghabhaidakaṃ, aikaṃ saṅghabhaidānuvattakaṃ, aikaṃ dubbacaṃ, aikaṃ kuladūsakaṃ, sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’’ti –

tikkhattuṃ yācāpaitvā tadanurūpāya kammavācāya parivāsau dātabbau|

aittha  ca ‘‘saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji nānāvatthukāyau’’tipi ‘‘saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji’’itipi aivaṃ pubbai vuttanayaina vatthuvasainapi  gauttavasainapi nāmavasainapi āpattivasainapi yaujaitvā kammaṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭatiyaiva, tasmā na idha visuṃ kammavācaṃ yaujaitvā dassayissāma pubbai sabbāpattisādhāraṇaṃ katvā yaujaitvā dassitāya aiva kammavācāya nānāvatthukāhipi āpattīhi vuṭṭhānasambhavatau sāyaivaittha kammavācā alanti|

missakasamaudhānaparivāsakathā niṭṭhitā|

246. sacai kauci bhikkhu parivasantau vibbhamati, sāmaṇairau vā hauti, vibbhamantassa sāmaṇairassa ca parivāsau na ruhati| sau cai puna upasampaj‍jati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ, yau parivāsau din‍nau, sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau, suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau| sacaipi mānattārahau mānattaṃ carantau abbhānārahau vā vibbhamati, sāmaṇairau vā hauti, sau cai puna upasampaj‍jati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ, yau parivāsau din‍nau, sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau, suparivutthau, yaṃ mānattaṃ din‍naṃ, sudin‍naṃ, yaṃ mānattaṃ ciṇṇaṃ, taṃ suciṇṇaṃ, sau bhikkhu abbhaitabbau|

sacai kauci bhikkhu parivasantau ummattakau hauti khittacittau vaidanāṭṭau, ummattakassa khittacittassa vaidanāṭṭassa ca parivāsau na ruhati| sau cai puna anummattakau hauti akhittacittau avaidanāṭṭau, tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ, yau parivāsau din‍nau, sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau, suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau| mānattārahādīsupi aisaiva nayau|

sacai kauci parivasantau ukkhittakau hauti, ukkhittakassa parivāsau na ruhati| sacai puna ausārīyati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ, yau parivāsau din‍nau, sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau, suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau| mānattārahādīsupi aisaiva nayau|

sacai kassaci bhikkhunau itthiliṅgaṃ pātubhavati, tassa sāyaiva upajjhā, sāyaiva upasampadā, puna upajjhā na gahaitabbā, upasampadā ca na kātabbā, bhikkhuupasampadatau pabhuti yāva vassagaṇanā, sāyaiva vassagaṇanā, na itau paṭṭhāya vassagaṇanā kātabbā| appatirūpaṃ dānissā bhikkhūnaṃ majjhai vasituṃ, tasmā bhikkhunupassayaṃ gantvā bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ vasitabbaṃ| yā daisanāgāminiyau vā vuṭṭhānagāminiyau vā āpattiyau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīhi  sādhāraṇā, tāsaṃ bhikkhunīhi kātabbaṃ, vinayakammamaiva bhikkhunīnaṃ santikai kātabbaṃ| yā pana bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīhi asādhāraṇā suk‍kavissaṭṭhiādikā āpattiyau, tāhi āpattīhi anāpatti, liṅgai parivattai tā āpattiyau vuṭṭhitāva haunti, puna pakatiliṅgai uppan‍naipi tāhi āpattīhi tassa anāpattiyaiva| bhikkhuniyā purisaliṅgai pātubhūtaipi aisaiva nayau| vuttañ‍caitaṃ –

‘‘taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau itthiliṅgaṃ pātubhūtaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi bhikkhavai taṃyaiva upajjhaṃ, taṃyaiva upasampadaṃ, tāniyaiva vassāni bhikkhunīhi saṅgamituṃ, yā āpattiyau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīhi sādhāraṇā, tā āpattiyau bhikkhunīnaṃ santikai vuṭṭhātuṃ| yā āpattiyau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīhi asādhāraṇā, tāhi āpattīhi anāpatti|

‘‘taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarissā bhikkhuniyā purisaliṅgaṃ pātubhūtaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi bhikkhavai taṃyaiva upajjhaṃ, taṃyaiva upasampadaṃ, tāniyaiva vassāni bhikkhūhi saṅgamituṃ, yā āpattiyau bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūhi sādhāraṇā, tā āpattiyau bhikkhūnaṃ santikai vuṭṭhātuṃ| yā āpattiyau bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūhi asādhāraṇā, tāhi āpattīhi anāpattī’’ti (pārā॰ 69)|

247. ayaṃ panaittha pāḷimuttakavinicchayau (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.69) – imaisu dvīsu liṅgaisu purisaliṅgaṃ uttamaṃ, itthiliṅgaṃ hīnaṃ, tasmā purisaliṅgaṃ balavaakusalaina antaradhāyati, itthiliṅgaṃ dubbalakusalaina patiṭṭhāti| itthiliṅgaṃ pana antaradhāyantaṃ dubbalaakusalaina antaradhāyati, purisaliṅgaṃ balavakusalaina patiṭṭhāti| aivaṃ ubhayampi akusalaina antaradhāyati, kusalaina paṭilabbhati|

tattha  sacai dvin‍naṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aikatau sajjhāyaṃ vā dhammasākacchaṃ vā katvā aikāgārai nipaj‍jitvā niddaṃ auk‍kantānaṃ aikassa itthiliṅgaṃ pātubhavati, ubhin‍nampi sahasaiyyāpatti hauti| sau cai paṭibujjhitvā attanau vippakāraṃ disvā dukkhī dummanau rattibhāgaiyaiva itarassa āraucaiyya, taina samassāsaitabbau ‘‘hautu mā cintayittha, vaṭṭassaivaisau dausau, sammāsambuddhaina dvāraṃ din‍naṃ, bhikkhu vā hautu bhikkhunī vā, anāvaṭau dhammau, avāritau saggamaggau’’ti| samassāsaitvā aivaṃ vattabbaṃ ‘‘tumhaihi bhikkhunupassayaṃ gantuṃ vaṭṭati, atthi pana tai kāci sandiṭṭhā  bhikkhuniyau’’ti| sacassā haunti tādisā bhikkhuniyau, ‘‘atthī’’ti, nau cai haunti, ‘‘natthī’’ti vatvā sau bhikkhu vattabbau ‘‘mama saṅgahaṃ karautha, idāni maṃ paṭhamaṃ bhikkhunupassayaṃ naithā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā taṃ gahaitvā tassā vā sandiṭṭhānaṃ attanau vā sandiṭṭhānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ santikaṃ gantabbaṃ| gacchantaina ca na aikakaina gantabbaṃ, catūhi pañ‍cahi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ jautikañ‍ca kattaradaṇḍakañ‍ca gahaitvā saṃvidahanaṃ parimaucaitvā ‘‘mayaṃ asukaṃ nāma ṭhānaṃ gacchāmā’’ti gantabbaṃ| sacai bahigāmai dūrai vihārau hauti, antarāmaggai gāmantaranadīpārarattivippavāsagaṇaauhīyanāpattīhi anāpatti| bhikkhunupassayaṃ gantvā tā bhikkhuniyau vattabbā ‘‘asukaṃ nāma bhikkhuṃ jānāthā’’ti? ‘‘āma, ayyā’’ti| tassa itthiliṅgaṃ pātubhūtaṃ, saṅgahaṃ dānissā karauthāti| tā cai ‘‘sādhu ayyā, idāni mayampi sajjhāyissāma, dhammaṃ saussāma, gacchatha tumhai’’ti vatvā saṅgahaṃ karaunti, ārādhikā ca haunti saṅgāhikā laj‍jiniyau, tā kaupaitvā añ‍ñattha na gantabbaṃ| gacchati cai, gāmantaranadīpārarattivippavāsagaṇaauhīyanāpattīhi na muc‍cati|

sacai pana laj‍jiniyau haunti, na saṅgāhikāyau, añ‍ñattha gantuṃ labbhati| sacaipi alaj‍jiniyau haunti, saṅgahaṃ pana karaunti, tāpi paric‍cajitvā añ‍ñattha gantuṃ labbhati| sacai laj‍jiniyau ca saṅgāhikā ca, ñātikā na haunti, āsan‍nagāmai pana añ‍ñā ñātikā haunti paṭijagganikā, tāsampi santikaṃ gantuṃ vaṭṭatīti vadanti| gantvā sacai bhikkhubhāvaipi nissayapaṭipan‍nau, patirūpāya bhikkhuniyā santikai nissayau gahaitabbau, mātikā vā vinayau vā uggahitau suggahitau, puna uggaṇhanakāraṇaṃ natthi| sacai bhikkhubhāvaipi parisāvacarau, tassa santikaiyaiva upasampan‍nā  sūpasampan‍nā, añ‍ñassa santikai nissayau gahaitabbau| pubbai taṃ nissāya vasantaihipi añ‍ñassa santikai nissayau gahaitabbau| paripuṇṇavassasāmaṇairainapi añ‍ñassa santikai upajjhā gahaitabbā|

yaṃ panassa bhikkhubhāvai adhiṭṭhitaṃ ticīvarañ‍ca pattau ca, taṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ vijahati, puna adhiṭṭhātabbaṃ| saṅkac‍cikā ca udakasāṭikā ca gahaitabbā| yaṃ atiraikacīvaraṃ vā atiraikapattau vā vinayakammaṃ katvā ṭhapitau hauti, tampi sabbaṃ vinayakammaṃ vijahati, puna kātabbaṃ| paṭiggahitatailamadhuphāṇitādīnipi paṭiggahaṇaṃ vijahanti| sacai paṭiggahaṇatau sattamai divasai liṅgaṃ parivattati, puna paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaṃ vaṭṭati| yaṃ pana bhikkhukālai añ‍ñassa bhikkhunau santakaṃ paṭiggahitaṃ, taṃ paṭiggahaṇaṃ na vijahati| yampi ubhin‍naṃ sādhāraṇaṃ avibhajitvā  ṭhapitaṃ, taṃ pakatattau rakkhati| yaṃ pana vibhattaṃ aitassaiva santakaṃ, taṃ paṭiggahaṇaṃ vijahati| vuttañ‍caitaṃ parivārai –

‘‘tailaṃ madhu phāṇitañ‍cāpi sappiṃ, sāmaṃ gahaitvā nikkhipaiyya|

avītivattai sattāhai, sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jantassa āpatti|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā’’ti|| (pari॰ 480)|

idañhi liṅgaparivattanaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| paṭiggahaṇaṃ nāma liṅgaparivattanaina, kālakiriyāya, sikkhāpac‍cakkhānaina, hīnāyāvattanaina, anupasampan‍nassa dānaina, anapaikkhavissaj‍janaina, acchinditvā gahaṇaina ca vijahati| tasmā sacaipi harītakakhaṇḍampi paṭiggahaitvā ṭhapitamatthi, sabbamassa paṭiggahaṇaṃ vijahati| bhikkhuvihārai pana yaṃ kiñ‍cissā santakaṃ paṭiggahaitvā vā appaṭiggahaitvā vā ṭhapitaṃ, sabbassa sāva issarā, āharāpaitvā gahaitabbaṃ| yaṃ panaittha thāvaraṃ tassā santakaṃ saināsanaṃ vā uparaupakā vā, tai yassicchati, tassa dātabbā| tairasasu sammutīsu yā bhikkhukālai laddhā sammuti, sabbā paṭippassambhati, purimikāya saināsanaggāhau paṭippassambhati| sacai pacchimikāya saināsanai gahitai liṅgaṃ parivattati, bhikkhusaṅghau cassā uppan‍nalābhaṃ dātukāmau hauti, apalaukaitvā dātabbau|

sacai bhikkhunīhi sādhāraṇāya paṭicchan‍nāya āpattiyā parivasantassa liṅgaṃ parivattati, puna pakkhamānattamaiva dātabbaṃ| sacai mānattaṃ carantassa parivattati, puna pakkhamānattamaiva dātabbaṃ| sacai ciṇṇamānattassa parivattati, bhikkhunīhi abbhānakammaṃ kātabbaṃ| sacai akusalavipākai parikkhīṇai pakkhamānattakālai punadaiva liṅgaṃ parivattati, chārattaṃ mānattamaiva dātabbaṃ| sacai ciṇṇai pakkhamānattai parivattati, bhikkhūhi abbhānakammaṃ kātabbanti|

bhikkhuniyā liṅgaparivattanaipi vuttanayainaiva sabbau vinicchayau vaiditabbau| ayaṃ pana visaisau – sacai bhikkhunikālai āpan‍nā sañ‍carittāpatti paṭicchan‍nā hauti, parivāsadānaṃ natthi, chārattaṃ mānattamaiva dātabbaṃ| sacai pakkhamānattaṃ carantiyā liṅgaṃ parivattati, na tainatthau, chārattaṃ mānattamaiva dātabbaṃ| sacai ciṇṇamānattāya parivattati, puna mānattaṃ adatvā bhikkhūhi abbhaitabbau| atha bhikkhūhi mānattai adin‍nai puna liṅgaṃ parivattati, bhikkhunīhi pakkhamānattamaiva dātabbaṃ| atha chārattaṃ mānattaṃ carantassa puna parivattati, pakkhamānattamaiva dātabbaṃ| ciṇṇamānattassa pana liṅgaparivattai jātai bhikkhunīhi abbhānakammaṃ kātabbaṃ| puna parivattai  ca liṅgai bhikkhunibhāvai ṭhitāyapi yā āpattiyau pubbai paṭippassaddhā, tā suppaṭippassaddhā aivāti|

248. itau paraṃ pārivāsikādīnaṃ vattaṃ dassayissāma – pārivāsikaina (cūḷava॰ aṭṭha॰ 76) bhikkhunā upajjhāyaina hutvā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, vattaṃ nikkhipitvā pana upasampādaituṃ vaṭṭati| ācariyaina hutvāpi kammavācā na sāvaitabbā, añ‍ñasmiṃ asati vattaṃ nikkhipitvā sāvaituṃ vaṭṭati| āgantukānaṃ nissayau na dātabbau| yaihipi pakatiyāva nissayau gahitau, tai vattabbā ‘‘ahaṃ vinayakammaṃ karaumi, asukatthairassa nāma santikai nissayaṃ gaṇhatha, mayhaṃ vattaṃ mā karautha, mā maṃ gāmappavaisanaṃ āpucchathā’’ti| sacai aivaṃ vuttaipi karauntiyaiva, vāritakālatau paṭṭhāya karauntaisupi anāpatti| añ‍ñau sāmaṇairaupi na gahaitabbau, upajjhaṃ datvā gahitasāmaṇairaupi vattabbau ‘‘ahaṃ vinayakammaṃ karaumi, mayhaṃ vattaṃ mā karautha, mā maṃ gāmappavaisanaṃ āpucchathā’’ti| sacai aivaṃ vuttaipi karauntiyaiva, vāritakālatau paṭṭhāya karauntaisupi anāpatti| na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā, tasmā bhikkhusaṅghassa vattabbaṃ ‘‘bhantai, ahaṃ vinayakammaṃ karaumi, bhikkhunauvādakaṃ  jānāthā’’ti| paṭibalassa vā bhikkhussa bhārau kātabbau| āgatā bhikkhuniyau ‘‘saṅghassa santikaṃ gacchatha, saṅghau vau auvādadāyakaṃ jānissatī’’ti vā ‘‘ahaṃ vinayakammaṃ karaumi, asukabhikkhussa nāma santikaṃ gacchatha, sau vau auvādaṃ dassatī’’ti vā vattabbā|

yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina parivāsau din‍nau hauti, sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā tādisikā tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā, kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā, na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, palibaudhatthāya vā pak‍kausanatthāya vā savacanīyaṃ na kātabbaṃ| palibaudhatthāya hi karauntau ‘‘ahaṃ āyasmantaṃ imasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ savacanīyaṃ karaumi, imamhā āvāsā parampi mā pak‍kama, yāva na taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ hautī’’ti aivaṃ karauti, pak‍kausanatthāya karauntau ‘‘ahaṃ taṃ savacanīyaṃ karaumi, aihi mayā saddhiṃ vinayadharānaṃ sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchāhī’’ti aivaṃ karauti, tadubhayampi na kātabbaṃ| vihārai jaiṭṭhakaṭṭhānaṃ na kātabbaṃ, pātimaukkhuddaisakaina vā dhammajjhaisakaina vā na bhavitabbaṃ, napi tairasasu sammutīsu aikasammutivasainapi issariyakammaṃ kātabbaṃ, ‘‘karautu mai āyasmā aukāsaṃ, ahaṃ taṃ vattukāmau’’ti aivaṃ pakatattassa aukāsau na kāraitabbau, vatthunā vā āpattiyā vā na caudaitabbau, ‘‘ayaṃ tai dausau’’ti na sāraitabbau, bhikkhūhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ yaujaitvā  kalahau na kāraitabbau, saṅghatthairaina hutvā pakatattassa bhikkhunau puratau na gantabbaṃ na nisīditabbaṃ, dvādasahatthaṃ upacāraṃ muñ‍citvā aikakainaiva gantabbañ‍caiva nisīditabbañ‍ca, yau hauti saṅghassa āsanapariyantau saiyyāpariyantau vihārapariyantau, sau tassa dātabbau|

tattha āsanapariyantau nāma bhattaggādīsu saṅghanavakāsanaṃ, svassa dātabbau, tattha nisīditabbaṃ| saiyyāpariyantau nāma saiyyānaṃ pariyantau sabbalāmakaṃ mañ‍capīṭhaṃ| ayañhi vassaggaina attanau pattaṭṭhānai saiyyaṃ gahaituṃ na labhati, sabbabhikkhūhi vicinitvā gahitāvasaisā maṅgulagūthabharitā vaittalatādivinaddhā lāmakasaiyyāvassa dātabbā| yathā ca saiyyā, aivaṃ vasanaāvāsaupi vassaggaina attanau pattaṭṭhānai tassa na vaṭṭati, sabbabhikkhūhi vicinitvā gahitāvasaisā pana rajauhatabhūmi jatukamūsikabharitā  paṇṇasālā assa dātabbā| sacai pakatattā sabbai rukkhamūlikā abbhaukāsikā ca haunti, chan‍naṃ na upainti, sabbaipi aitaihi vissaṭṭhāvāsā nāma haunti, taisu yaṃ icchati, taṃ labhati|

vassūpanāyikadivasai pac‍cayaṃ aikapassai ṭhatvā vassaggaina gaṇhituṃ labhati, saināsanaṃ na labhati, nibaddhavassāvāsikaṃ saināsanaṃ gaṇhitu kāmaina vattaṃ nikkhipitvā gahaitabbaṃ| ñātipavāritaṭṭhānai ‘‘aittakai bhikkhū gahaitvā āgacchathā’’ti nimantitaina ‘‘bhantai, asukaṃ nāma kulaṃ bhikkhū nimantaisi, aitha, tattha gacchāmā’’ti aivaṃ saṃvidhāya bhikkhūnaṃ puraisamaṇaina vā pacchāsamaṇaina vā hutvā kulāni na upasaṅkamitabbāni, ‘‘bhantai, asukasmiṃ nāma gāmai manussā bhikkhūnaṃ āgamanaṃ icchanti, sādhu vatassa, sacai taisaṃ saṅgahaṃ karaiyyāthā’’ti aivaṃ panassa vinayapariyāyaina kathaituṃ vaṭṭati| āgatāgatānaṃ āraucaituṃ harāyamānaina ārañ‍ñikadhutaṅgaṃ na samādātabbaṃ| yainapi pakatiyā samādin‍naṃ, taina dutiyaṃ bhikkhuṃ gahaitvā arañ‍ñai aruṇaṃ uṭṭhāpaitabbaṃ, na aikakaina vatthabbaṃ| tathā bhattaggādīsu āsanapariyantai nisaj‍jāya harāyamānaina piṇḍapātikadhutaṅgampi na samādātabbaṃ| yau pana pakatiyāva piṇḍapātikau, tassa paṭisaidhau natthi, na ca tappac‍cayā piṇḍapātau nīharāpaitabbau ‘‘mā maṃ jāniṃsū’’ti| nīhaṭabhattau hutvā vihāraiyaiva nisīditvā bhuñ‍jantau ‘‘rattiyau gaṇayissāmi, gacchatau mai bhikkhuṃ disvā anāraucaintassa ratticchaidau siyā’’ti iminā kāraṇaina piṇḍapātau na nīharāpaitabbau, ‘‘mā maṃ aikabhikkhupi jānātū’’ti ca iminā ajjhāsayaina vihārai sāmaṇairaihi pacāpaitvā bhuñ‍jitumpi na labhati, gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisitabbamaiva| gilānassa pana navakammaācariyupajjhāyakic‍cādipasutassa vā vihāraiyaiva acchituṃ vaṭṭati|

sacaipi  gāmai anaikasatā bhikkhū vicaranti, na sak‍kā hauti āraucaituṃ, gāmakāvāsaṃ gantvā sabhāgaṭṭhānai vasituṃ vaṭṭati| yasmā ‘‘pārivāsikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā āgantukaina āraucaitabbaṃ, āgantukassa āraucaitabbaṃ, upausathai āraucaitabbaṃ, pavāraṇāya āraucaitabbaṃ, sacai gilānau hauti, dūtainapi āraucaitabba’’nti (cūḷava॰ 76) vuttaṃ, tasmā kañ‍ci vihāraṃ gataina āgantukaina tattha bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ| sacai sabbai aikaṭṭhānai ṭhitai passati, aikaṭṭhānai ṭhitainaiva āraucaitabbaṃ| atha rukkhamūlādīsu visuṃ ṭhitā haunti, tattha tattha gantvā āraucaitabbaṃ, sañ‍cic‍ca anāraucaintassa ratticchaidau ca hauti, vattabhaidai ca duk‍kaṭaṃ| atha vicinantau aikac‍cai na passati, ratticchaidauva hauti, na vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭaṃ|

āgantukassapi attanau vasanavihāraṃ āgatassa aikassa vā bahūnaṃ vā vuttanayainaiva āraucaitabbaṃ, ratticchaidavattabhaidāpi caittha vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbā| sacai āgantukā muhuttaṃ vissamitvā vā avissamitvā aiva vā vihāramajjhaina gacchanti, taisampi āraucaitabbaṃ| sacai tassa ajānantassaiva gacchanti, ayañ‍ca gatakālai jānāti, gantvā āraucaitabbaṃ, sampāpuṇituṃ vā sāvaituṃ vā asak‍kauntassa ratticchaidauva hauti, na vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭaṃ| yaipi antauvihāraṃ appavisitvā upacārasīmaṃ auk‍kamitvā gacchanti, ayañ‍ca naisaṃ chattasaddaṃ vā uk‍kāsitasaddaṃ vā khipitasaddaṃ vā sutvā āgantukabhāvaṃ jānāti, gantvā āraucaitabbaṃ, gatakālai jānantainapi anubandhitvā āraucaitabbamaiva, sampāpuṇituṃ asak‍kauntassa ratticchaidauva hauti, na vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭaṃ| yaupi rattiṃ āgantvā rattiṃyaiva gacchati, saupissa ratticchaidaṃ karauti, añ‍ñātattā pana vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭaṃ natthi| sacai ajānitvāva abbhānaṃ karauti, akatamaiva hautīti kurundiyaṃ vuttaṃ, tasmā adhikā rattiyau gahaitvā kātabbaṃ| ayaṃ apaṇṇakapaṭipadā|

nadīādīsu nāvāya gacchantampi paratīrai ṭhitampi ākāsai gacchantampi pabbatatalaarañ‍ñādīsu dūrai ṭhitampi bhikkhuṃ disvā sacai ‘‘bhikkhū’’ti vavatthānaṃ atthi, nāvādīhi gantvā vā mahāsaddaṃ katvā vā vaigaina anubandhitvā vā āraucaitabbaṃ, anāraucaintassa ratticchaidau caiva vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭañ‍ca| sacai vāyamantaupi sampāpuṇituṃ vā sāvaituṃ vā na sak‍kauti, ratticchaidauva hauti, na vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭaṃ| saṅghasainābhayatthairau pana visayāvisayaina kathaiti ‘‘visayai kira anāraucaintassa ratticchaidau caiva vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭañ‍ca hauti, avisayai pana ubhayampi natthī’’ti| karavīkatissatthairau ‘‘samaṇau ayanti vavatthānamaiva pamāṇaṃ| sacaipi avisayau hauti, vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭamaiva natthi, ratticchaidau pana hautiyaivā’’ti āha|

upausathadivasai  ‘‘upausathaṃ sampāpuṇissāmā’’ti āgantukā bhikkhū āgacchanti, iddhiyā gacchantāpi upausathabhāvaṃ ñatvā autaritvā upausathaṃ karaunti, tasmā āgantukasaudhanatthaṃ upausathadivasaipi āraucaitabbaṃ| pavāraṇāyapi aisaiva nayau| gantuṃ asamatthaina gilānaina bhikkhuṃ paisaitvā āraucāpaitabbaṃ, anupasampan‍naṃ paisaituṃ na vaṭṭati|

na pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā abhikkhukau nānāsaṃvāsakaihi vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau añ‍ñatra pakatattaina añ‍ñatra antarāyā| yattha hi aikaupi bhikkhu natthi, tattha na vasitabbaṃ| na hi tattha vuttharattiyau gaṇanūpikā haunti| dasavidhai antarāyai pana sacaipi rattiyau gaṇanūpikā na haunti, antarāyatau parimuc‍canatthāya gantabbamaiva| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘añ‍ñatra antarāyā’’ti| nānāsaṃvāsakaihi saddhiṃ vinayakammaṃ kātuṃ na vaṭṭati, taisaṃ anāraucanaipi ratticchaidau natthi, abhikkhukāvāsasadisamaiva hauti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘nānāsaṃvāsakaihi vā sabhikkhukau’’ti|

na pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ| tattha āvāsau nāma vasanatthāya katasaināsanaṃ| anāvāsau nāma caitiyagharaṃ baudhigharaṃ sammuñ‍janīaṭṭakau dāruaṭṭakau pānīyamāḷau vac‍cakuṭi dvārakauṭṭhakauti aivamādi| ‘‘aitaisu yattha katthaci aikacchan‍nai chadanatau udakapatanaṭṭhānaparicchin‍nai aukāsai ukkhittakauva vasituṃ na labhati, pārivāsikau pana antauāvāsaiyaiva na labhatī’’ti mahāpac‍cariyaṃ vuttaṃ| mahāaṭṭhakathāyaṃ avisaisaina ‘‘udakapātaina vārita’’nti vuttaṃ| kurundiyaṃ pana ‘‘aitaisu aittakaisu pañ‍cavaṇṇacchadanabaddhaṭṭhānaisu pārivāsikassa ca ukkhittakassa ca pakatattaina saddhiṃ udakapātaina vārita’’nti vuttaṃ| tasmā nānūpacāraipi aikacchan‍nai na vaṭṭati| sacai panaittha tadahupasampan‍naipi pakatattai paṭhamaṃ pavisitvā nipan‍naipi saṭṭhivassikaupi pārivāsikau pacchā pavisitvā jānantau nipaj‍jati, ratticchaidau caiva vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭañ‍ca, ajānantassa ratticchaidauva, na vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭaṃ| sacai pana tasmiṃ nisin‍nai pacchā pakatattau  pavisitvā nipaj‍jati, pārivāsikau ca jānāti, ratticchaidau caiva vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭañ‍ca| nau cai jānāti, ratticchaidauva, na vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭaṃ|

pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ tadahupasampan‍nampi disvā āsanā vuṭṭhātabbaṃ, vuṭṭhāya ca ‘‘ahaṃ iminā sukhanisin‍nau vuṭṭhāpitau’’ti parammukhainapi na gantabbaṃ, ‘‘idaṃ ācariya āsanaṃ, aittha nisīdathā’’ti aivaṃ pakatattau bhikkhu āsanaina nimantaitabbauyaiva| navakaina  pana ‘‘mahāthairaṃ aubaddhaṃ karaumī’’ti pārivāsikatthairassa santikaṃ na gantabbaṃ| pārivāsikaina pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ na aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, dvādasahatthaṃ pana upacāraṃ muñ‍citvā nisīdituṃ vaṭṭati| pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina saddhiṃ na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ| aittha pana akataparicchaidāya bhūmiyā caṅkamantai paricchaidaṃ katvā vālukaṃ ākiritvā ālambanaṃ yaujaitvā katacaṅkamai nīcaipi na caṅkamitabbaṃ, kau pana vādau iṭṭhakacayaina sampan‍nai vaidikāparikkhittai| sacai pana pākāraparikkhittau hauti, dvārakauṭṭhakayuttau pabbatantaravanantaragumbantaraisu vā suppaṭicchan‍nau, tādisai caṅkamai caṅkamituṃ vaṭṭati, appaṭicchan‍naipi upacāraṃ muñ‍citvā vaṭṭati|

pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā pārivāsikavuḍḍhataraina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina mānattārahaina mānattacārikaina abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ| aittha pana sacai vuḍḍhatarai pārivāsikai paṭhamaṃ nipan‍nai itarau jānantau pacchā nipaj‍jati, ratticchaidau cassa hauti, vattabhaidai ca duk‍kaṭaṃ| vuḍḍhatarassa pana ratticchaidauva, na vattabhaidai duk‍kaṭaṃ| ajānitvā nipaj‍jati, dvin‍nampi vattabhaidau natthi, ratticchaidau pana hauti| atha navakapārivāsikai paṭhamaṃ nipan‍nai vuḍḍhatarau pacchā nipaj‍jati, navakau ca jānāti, ratti cassa chij‍jati, vattabhaidai ca duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti| vuḍḍhatarassa ratticchaidauva, na vattabhaidau| nau cai jānāti, dvin‍nampi vattabhaidau natthi, ratticchaidau pana hauti| sacai apacchāpurimaṃ nipaj‍janti, vuḍḍhatarassa ratticchaidauva, itarassa vattabhaidaupīti kurundiyaṃ vuttaṃ|

dvai  pārivāsikā samavassā, aikau paṭhamaṃ nipan‍nau, aikau jānantauva pacchā nipaj‍jati, ratti cassa chij‍jati, vattabhaidai ca duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭhamaṃ nipan‍nassa ratticchaidauva, na vattabhaidau| sacai pacchā nipaj‍jantaupi na jānāti, dvin‍nampi vattabhaidau natthi, ratticchaidau pana hauti| sacai dvaipi apacchāpurimaṃ nipaj‍janti, dvin‍nampi ratticchaidauyaiva, na vattabhaidau| sacai hi dvai pārivāsikā aikatau vasaiyyuṃ, tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa ajjhācāraṃ ñatvā agāravā vā vippaṭisārinau vā hutvā taṃ vā āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ tatau pāpiṭṭhataraṃ vā, vibbhamaiyyuṃ vā, tasmā naisaṃ sahasaiyyā sabbapakāraina paṭikkhittā| mūlāyapaṭikassanārahādayau caittha pārivāsikānaṃ pakatattaṭṭhānai ṭhitāti vaiditabbā| tasmā pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina  mānattārahaina mānattacārikaina abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ na aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāya nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāya caṅkamantai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sāditabbaṃ pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ āsanābhihārau saiyyābhihārau pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ pādakathalikaṃ pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ, yau sādiyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti (cūḷava॰ 75) vacanatau pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ṭhapaitvā navakataraṃ pārivāsikaṃ avasaisānaṃ antamasau mūlāyapaṭikassanārahādīnampi abhivādanādiṃ sādiyantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ, saddhivihārikānampi sādiyantassa duk‍kaṭamaiva| tasmā tai vattabbā ‘‘ahaṃ vinayakammaṃ karaumi, mayhaṃ vattaṃ mā karautha, mā maṃ gāmappavaisanaṃ āpucchathā’’ti| sacai saddhāpabbajitā kulaputtā ‘‘tumhai, bhantai, tumhākaṃ vinayakammaṃ karauthā’’ti vatvā vattaṃ karaunti, gāmappavaisanampi āpucchantiyaiva, vāritakālatau paṭṭhāya anāpatti|

‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mithū yathāvuḍḍhaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ āsanābhihāraṃ saiyyābhihāraṃ pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ pādakathalikaṃ pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ nahānai piṭṭhiparikamma’’nti  (cūḷava॰ 75) vacanatau pana pārivāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ yau yau vuḍḍhau, taina taina navakatarassa abhivādanādiṃ sādituṃ vaṭṭati|

‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pañ‍ca yathāvuḍḍhaṃ upausathaṃ pavāraṇaṃ vassikasāṭikaṃ auṇaujanaṃ bhatta’’nti (cūḷava॰ 75) vacanatau imāni upausathādīni pañ‍ca pakatattaihipi saddhiṃ vuḍḍhapaṭipāṭiyā kātuṃ vaṭṭati, tasmā (cūḷava॰ aṭṭha॰ 75) pātimaukkhai uddissamānai hatthapāsai nisīdituṃ vaṭṭati| mahāpac‍cariyaṃ pana ‘‘pāḷiyā anisīditvā pāḷiṃ vihāya hatthapāsaṃ amuñ‍cantaina nisīditabba’’nti vuttaṃ| pārisuddhiupausathai kariyamānai saṅghanavakaṭṭhānai nisīditvā tatthaiva nisin‍naina attanau pāḷiyā pārisuddhiupausathau kātabbau| pavāraṇāyapi saṅghanavakaṭṭhānai nisīditvā tatthaiva nisin‍naina attanau pāḷiyā pavāraitabbaṃ| saṅghaina ghaṇṭiṃ paharitvā bhājiyamānaṃ vassikasāṭikampi attanau pattaṭṭhānai gahaituṃ vaṭṭati|

auṇaujananti  vissaj‍janaṃ vuc‍cati| sacai hi pārivāsikassa dvai tīṇi uddaisabhattādīni pāpuṇanti, añ‍ñā cassa puggalikabhattapac‍cāsā hauti, tāni paṭipāṭiyā gahaitvā ‘‘bhantai, haiṭṭhā gāhaitha, aj‍ja mayhaṃ bhattapac‍cāsā atthi, svaiva gaṇhissāmī’’ti vatvā vissaj‍jaitabbāni| aivaṃ tāni punadivasaisu gaṇhituṃ labhati| ‘‘punadivasai sabbapaṭhamaṃ tassa dātabba’’nti kurundiyaṃ vuttaṃ| yadi pana na gaṇhāti na vissaj‍jaiti, punadivasai na labhati| idaṃ auṇaujanaṃ nāma pārivāsikassaiva audissa anuñ‍ñātaṃ| kasmā? tassa hi saṅghanavakaṭṭhānai nisin‍nassa bhattaggai yāgukhaj‍jakādīni pāpuṇanti vā na vā, tasmā ‘‘sau bhikkhāhāraina mā kilamitthā’’ti idamassa saṅgahakaraṇatthāya audissa anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

bhattanti āgatāgataihi vuḍḍhapaṭipāṭiyā gahaitvā gantabbaṃ vihārai saṅghassa catussālabhattaṃ| aitaṃ yathāvuḍḍhaṃ labhati, pāḷiyā pana gantuṃ vā ṭhātuṃ vā na labhati, tasmā pāḷitau ausak‍kitvā hatthapāsai ṭhitaina hatthaṃ pasāraitvā yathā sainau nipatitvā gaṇhāti, aivaṃ gaṇhitabbaṃ| ārāmikasamaṇuddaisaihi āharāpaituṃ na labhati| sacai sayamaiva āharanti, vaṭṭati| rañ‍ñau mahāpaiḷabhattaipi aisaiva nayau| catussālabhattai pana sacai auṇaujanaṃ kattukāmau hauti, attanau atthāya ukkhittai  piṇḍai ‘‘aj‍ja mai bhattaṃ atthi, svaiva gaṇhissāmī’’ti vattabbaṃ| ‘‘punadivasai dvai piṇḍai labhatī’’ti mahāpac‍cariyaṃ vuttaṃ| uddaisabhattādīnipi pāḷitau ausak‍kitvāva gahaitabbāni, yattha pana nisīdāpaitvā parivisanti, tattha sāmaṇairānaṃ jaiṭṭhakaina, bhikkhūnaṃ saṅghanavakaina hutvā nisīditabbaṃ| idaṃ pārivāsikavattaṃ|

mūlāyapaṭikassanārahānaṃ mānattārahānaṃ mānattacārikānaṃ abbhānārahānañ‍ca idamaiva vattanti vaiditabbaṃ| mānattacārikassa vattai pana ‘‘daivasikaṃ āraucaitabba’’nti visaisau| ratticchaidaisu ca ‘‘tayau khau, upāli, pārivāsikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidā, sahavāsau vippavāsau anāraucanā’’ti (cūḷava॰ 83) vacanatau yvāyaṃ ‘‘pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai’’tiādinā nayaina vuttau sahavāsau, yau ca aikassaiva vāsau, yā cāyaṃ āgantukādīnaṃ anāraucanā, aitaisu tīsu aikainapi kāraṇaina pārivāsikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidau hauti|

mānattacārikassa pana ‘‘cattārau khau, upāli, mānattacārikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidā, sahavāsau, vippavāsau, anāraucanā, ūnai gaṇai caraṇa’’nti vacanatau imaisu catūsu kāraṇaisu aikainapi  ratticchaidau hauti| gaṇauti caittha cattārau vā atiraikā vā| tasmā sacaipi tīhi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ vasati, ratticchaidau hautiyaiva|

iti pāḷimuttakavinayavinicchayasaṅgahai

garukāpattivuṭṭhānavinicchayakathā samattā|

33. kammākammavinicchayakathā

249. kammākammanti  aittha (pari॰ 482-484) pana kammāni cattāri – apalaukanakammaṃ ñattikammaṃ ñattidutiyakammaṃ ñatticatutthakammanti| imāni cattāri kammāni katihākāraihi vipaj‍janti? pañ‍cahākāraihi vipaj‍janti – vatthutau vā ñattitau vā anussāvanatau vā sīmatau vā parisatau vā|

kathaṃ  vatthutau kammāni vipaj‍janti? sammukhākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ asammukhā karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| paṭipucchākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ apaṭipucchā karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| paṭiñ‍ñāya karaṇīyaṃ kammaṃ apaṭiñ‍ñāya karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| sativinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| amūḷhavinayārahassa tassa pāpiyasikakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| tassa pāpiyasikakammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| taj‍janīyakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| niyassakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| pabbājanīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| parivāsārahassa mūlāya paṭikassati, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| mūlāyapaṭikassanārahassa mānattaṃ daiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| mānattārahaṃ abbhaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| abbhānārahaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| anupausathai upausathaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| apavāraṇāya pavāraiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| paṇḍakaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| thaiyyasaṃvāsakaṃ, titthiyapak‍kantakaṃ, tiracchānagataṃ, mātughātakaṃ, pitughātakaṃ, arahantaghātakaṃ, bhikkhunidūsakaṃ, saṅghabhaidakaṃ, lauhituppādakaṃ, ubhataubyañ‍janakaṃ, ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| aivaṃ vatthutau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

kathaṃ ñattitau kammāni vipaj‍janti? pañ‍cahākāraihi ñattitau kammāni vipaj‍janti – vatthuṃ na parāmasati, saṅghaṃ na parāmasati, puggalaṃ na parāmasati, ñattiṃ na parāmasati, pacchā vā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti| imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi ñattitau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

kathaṃ  anussāvanatau kammāni vipaj‍janti? pañ‍cahākāraihi anussāvanatau kammāni vipaj‍janti – vatthuṃ na parāmasati, saṅghaṃ na parāmasati, puggalaṃ na parāmasati, sāvanaṃ hāpaiti, akālai vā sāvaiti| imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi anussāvanatau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

kathaṃ sīmatau kammāni vipaj‍janti? aikādasahi ākāraihi sīmatau kammāni vipaj‍janti – atikhuddakaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, atimahatiṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, khaṇḍanimittaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, chāyānimittaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, animittaṃ sīmaṃ  samman‍nati, bahisīmai ṭhitau sīmaṃ samman‍nati, nadiyā sīmaṃ samman‍nati, samuddai sīmaṃ samman‍nati, jātassarai sīmaṃ samman‍nati, sīmāya sīmaṃ sambhindati, sīmāya sīmaṃ ajjhauttharati| imaihi aikādasahi ākāraihi sīmatau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

kathaṃ parisatau kammāni vipaj‍janti? dvādasahi ākāraihi parisatau kammāni vipaj‍janti – catuvaggakaraṇīyai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai anāgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti, catuvaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti, catuvaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti|

pañ‍cavaggakaraṇai kammai…pai॰… dasavaggakaraṇai kammai…pai॰… vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, tai anāgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti, vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti, vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti| imaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi parisatau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

catuvaggakaraṇai kammai cattārau bhikkhū pakatattā kammappattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti, sau naiva kammappattau nāpi chandārahau, apica kammārahau| pañ‍cavaggakaraṇai kammai pañ‍ca bhikkhū pakatattā kammappattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti, sau naiva kammappattau nāpi chandārahau, apica kammārahau| dasavaggakaraṇai kammai dasa bhikkhū pakatattā kammappattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti, sau naiva kammappattau nāpi chandārahau, apica kammārahau| vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai vīsati bhikkhū pakatattā kammappattā, avasaisā  pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti, sau naiva kammappattau nāpi chandārahau, apica kammārahau|

250. apalaukanakammaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati? ñattikammaṃ, ñattidutiyakammaṃ, ñatticatutthakammaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati? apalaukanakammaṃ pañ‍ca ṭhānāni gacchati| ñattikammaṃ nava ṭhānāni gacchati| ñattidutiyakammaṃ satta ṭhānāni gacchati| ñatticatutthakammaṃ satta ṭhānāni gacchati|

apalaukanakammaṃ katamāni pañ‍ca ṭhānāni gacchati? ausāraṇaṃ nissāraṇaṃ bhaṇḍukammaṃ brahmadaṇḍaṃ kammalakkhaṇañ‍ñaiva pañ‍camaṃ| apalaukanakammaṃ imāni pañ‍ca ṭhānāni gacchati|

ñattikammaṃ katamāni nava ṭhānāni gacchati? ausāraṇaṃ nissāraṇaṃ upausathaṃ pavāraṇaṃ sammutiṃ dānaṃ paṭiggahaṇaṃ pac‍cuk‍kaḍḍhanaṃ kammalakkhaṇañ‍ñaiva navamaṃ| ñattikammaṃ imāni nava ṭhānāni gacchati|

ñattidutiyakammaṃ katamāni satta ṭhānāni gacchati? ausāraṇaṃ nissāraṇaṃ sammutiṃ dānaṃ uddharaṇaṃ daisanaṃ kammalakkhaṇañ‍ñaiva sattamaṃ| ñattidutiyakammaṃ imāni satta ṭhānāni gacchati|

ñatticatutthakammaṃ katamāni satta ṭhānāni gacchati? ausāraṇaṃ nissāraṇaṃ sammutiṃ dānaṃ niggahaṃ samanubhāsanaṃ kammalakkhaṇañ‍ñaiva sattamaṃ| ñatticatutthakammaṃ imāni satta ṭhānāni gacchati| ayaṃ tāva pāḷinayau|

251. ayaṃ panaittha āditau paṭṭhāya vinicchayakathā (pari॰ aṭṭha॰ 482) – apalaukanakammaṃ nāma sīmaṭṭhakasaṅghaṃ saudhaitvā chandārahānaṃ chandaṃ āharitvā samaggassa saṅghassa anumatiyā tikkhattuṃ sāvaitvā kattabbakammaṃ| ñattikammaṃ nāma vuttanayainaiva samaggassa saṅghassa anumatiyā aikāya ñattiyā kattabbakammaṃ| ñattidutiyakammaṃ nāma vuttanayainaiva samaggassa saṅghassa anumatiyā aikāya ñattiyā aikāya ca anussāvanāyāti aivaṃ ñattidutiyāya anussāvanāya  kattabbakammaṃ| ñatticatutthakammaṃ nāma vuttanayainaiva samaggassa saṅghassa anumatiyā aikāya ñattiyā tīhi ca anussāvanāhīti aivaṃ ñatticatutthāhi tīhi anussāvanāhi kattabbakammaṃ|

tatra  apalaukanakammaṃ apalaukaitvāva kātabbaṃ, ñattikammādivasaina na kātabbaṃ| ñattikammampi aikaṃ ñattiṃ ṭhapaitvāva kātabbaṃ, apalaukanakammādivasaina na kātabbaṃ| ñattidutiyakammaṃ pana apalaukaitvā kātabbampi akātabbampi atthi| tattha sīmāsammuti sīmāsamūhanaṃ kathinadānaṃ kathinuddhārau kuṭivatthudaisanā vihāravatthudaisanāti imāni chakammāni garukāni apalaukaitvā kātuṃ na vaṭṭati, ñattidutiyakammavācaṃ sāvaitvāva kātabbāni| avasaisā tairasa sammutiyau saināsanaggāhakamatakacīvaradānādisammutiyau cāti aitāni lahukakammāni, apalaukaitvāpi kātuṃ vaṭṭanti, ñattikammañatticatutthakammavasaina pana na kātabbamaiva| ‘‘ñatticatutthakammavasaina kayiramānaṃ daḷhataraṃ hauti, tasmā kātabba’’nti aikac‍cai vadanti| aivaṃ pana sati kammasaṅkarau hauti, tasmā na kātabbanti paṭikkhittamaiva| sacai pana akkharaparihīnaṃ vā padaparihīnaṃ vā duruttapadaṃ vā hauti, tassa saudhanatthaṃ punappunaṃ vattuṃ vaṭṭati| idaṃ akuppakammassa daḷhīkammaṃ hauti, kuppakammai kammaṃ hutvā tiṭṭhati| ñatticatutthakammaṃ ñattiñ‍ca tissau ca kammavācāyau sāvaitvāva kātabbaṃ, apalaukanakammādivasaina na kātabbaṃ|

sammukhākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ asammukhā karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammanti aittha pana atthi kammaṃ sammukhākaraṇīyaṃ, atthi kammaṃ asammukhākaraṇīyaṃ| tattha asammukhākaraṇīyaṃ nāma dūtainūpasampadā, pattanik‍kuj‍janaṃ, pattuk‍kuj‍janaṃ, ummattakassa bhikkhunau ummattakasammuti, saikkhānaṃ kulānaṃ saikkhasammuti, chan‍nassa bhikkhunau brahmadaṇḍau, daivadattassa pakāsanīyakammaṃ, apasādanīyaṃ dassaintassa bhikkhunau bhikkhunisaṅghaina kātabbaṃ avandiyakammanti aṭṭhavidhaṃ hauti| idaṃ aṭṭhavidhampi kammaṃ asammukhā kataṃ sukataṃ hauti akuppaṃ, saisāni sabbakammāni sammukhā aiva kātabbāni| saṅghasammukhatā dhammasammukhatā vinayasammukhatā puggalasammukhatāti imaṃ catubbidhaṃ sammukhāvinayaṃ upanaitvāva kātabbāni| aivaṃ katāni hi sukatāni haunti, aivaṃ akatāni panaitāni imaṃ sammukhāvinayasaṅkhātaṃ vatthuṃ vinā katattā vatthuvipan‍nāni nāma haunti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sammukhākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ asammukhā karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakamma’’nti| paṭipucchākaraṇīyādīsupi paṭipucchādikaraṇamaiva vatthu, taṃ vatthuṃ vinā katattā taisampi vatthuvipan‍natā vaiditabbā| apica ūnavīsativassaṃ vā antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍napubbaṃ vā aikādasasu  vā abhabbapuggalaisu añ‍ñataraṃ upasampādaintassapi vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ hauti| ayaṃ vatthutau kammavipattiyaṃ vinicchayau|

ñattitau vipattiyaṃ pana vatthuṃ na parāmasatīti yassa upasampadādikammaṃ karauti, taṃ na parāmasati, tassa nāmaṃ na gaṇhāti| ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ dhammarakkhitau āyasmatau buddharakkhitassa upasampadāpaikkhau’’ti vattabbai ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, āyasmatau buddharakkhitassa upasampadāpaikkhau’’ti vadati| aivaṃ vatthuṃ na parāmasati|

saṅghaṃ na parāmasatīti saṅghassa nāmaṃ na gaṇhāti| ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ dhammarakkhitau’’ti vattabbai ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, ayaṃ dhammarakkhitau’’ti vadati| aivaṃ saṅghaṃ na parāmasati|

puggalaṃ na parāmasatīti yau upasampadāpaikkhassa upajjhāyau, taṃ na parāmasati, tassa nāmaṃ na gaṇhāti| ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ dhammarakkhitau āyasmatau buddharakkhitassa upasampadāpaikkhau’’ti vattabbai ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ dhammarakkhitau upasampadāpaikkhau’’ti vadati| aivaṃ puggalaṃ na parāmasati|

ñattiṃ na parāmasatīti sabbaina sabbaṃ ñattiṃ na parāmasati, ñattidutiyakammai ñattiṃ aṭṭhapaitvā dvikkhattuṃ kammavācāya aiva anussāvanakammaṃ karauti, ñatticatutthakammaipi ñattiṃ aṭṭhapaitvā catukkhattuṃ kammavācāya aiva anussāvanakammaṃ karauti| aivaṃ ñattiṃ na parāmasati|

pacchā vā ñattiṃ ṭhapaitīti paṭhamaṃ kammavācāya anussāvanakammaṃ katvā ‘‘aisā ñattī’’ti vatvā ‘‘khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti vadati| aivaṃ pacchā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti| iti imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi ñattitau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

anussāvanatau vipattiyaṃ pana vatthuādīni tāva vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbāni| aivaṃ pana naisaṃ aparāmasanaṃ hauti – ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau’’ti paṭhamānussāvanāya vā ‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi| suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau’’ti dutiyatatiyānussāvanāsu vā ‘‘ayaṃ dhammarakkhitau āyasmatau buddharakkhitassa upasampadāpaikkhau’’ti vattabbai ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, āyasmatau buddharakkhitassā’’ti vadantau  vatthuṃ na parāmasati nāma| ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ dhammarakkhitau’’ti vattabbai ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, ayaṃ dhammarakkhitau’’ti vadantau saṅghaṃ na parāmasati nāma| ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ dhammarakkhitau āyasmatau buddharakkhitassā’’ti vattabbai ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau, ayaṃ dhammarakkhitau upasampadāpaikkhau’’ti vadantau puggalaṃ na parāmasati nāma|

sāvanaṃ hāpaitīti sabbaina sabbaṃ kammavācāya anussāvanaṃ na karauti, ñattidutiyakammai dvikkhattuṃ ñattimaiva ṭhapaiti, ñatticatutthakammai catukkhattuṃ ñattimaiva ṭhapaiti| aivaṃ sāvanaṃ hāpaiti| yaupi ñattidutiyakammai aikaṃ ñattiṃ ṭhapaitvā aikaṃ kammavācaṃ anussāvaintau akkharaṃ vā chaḍḍaiti, padaṃ vā duruttaṃ karauti, ayampi sāvanaṃ hāpaitiyaiva| ñatticatutthakammai pana aikaṃ ñattiṃ ṭhapaitvā sakimaiva vā dvikkhattuṃ vā kammavācāya anussāvanaṃ karauntaupi akkharaṃ vā padaṃ vā chaḍḍaintaupi duruttaṃ karauntaupi anussāvanaṃ hāpaitiyaivāti vaiditabbau|

252. ‘‘duruttaṃ karautī’’ti aittha pana ayaṃ vinicchayau| yau hi añ‍ñasmiṃ akkharai vattabbai añ‍ñaṃ vadati, ayaṃ duruttaṃ karauti nāma| tasmā kammavācaṃ karauntaina bhikkhunā yvāyaṃ –

‘‘sithilaṃ dhanitañ‍ca dīgharassaṃ, garukaṃ lahukañ‍ca niggahitaṃ|

sambandhaṃ vavatthitaṃ vimuttaṃ, dasadhā byañ‍janabuddhiyā pabhaidau’’ti|| –

vuttau, ayaṃ suṭṭhu upalakkhaitabbau| aittha hi sithilaṃ nāma pañ‍casu vaggaisu paṭhamatatiyaṃ| dhanitaṃ nāma taisvaiva dutiyacatutthaṃ| dīghanti dīghaina kālaina vattabbaākārādi| rassanti tatau upaḍḍhakālaina vattabbaakārādi| garukanti dīghamaiva, yaṃ vā ‘‘āyasmatau buddharakkhitatthairassa yassa nakkhamatī’’ti aivaṃ saṃyaugaparaṃ katvā vuc‍cati| lahukanti rassamaiva, yaṃ vā ‘‘āyasmatau buddharakkhitatthairassa yassa na khamatī’’ti aivaṃ asaṃyaugaparaṃ katvā vuc‍cati| niggahitanti yaṃ karaṇāni niggahaitvā avissaj‍jaitvā avivaṭaina mukhaina sānunāsikaṃ katvā vattabbaṃ| sambandhanti yaṃ parapadaina sambandhitvā ‘‘tuṇhissā’’ti vā ‘‘tuṇhassā’’ti vā vuc‍cati| vavatthitanti yaṃ parapadaina asambandhaṃ katvā vicchinditvā ‘‘tuṇhī assā’’ti vā ‘‘tuṇha assā’’ti vā vuc‍cati| vimuttanti yaṃ karaṇāni aniggahaitvā vissaj‍jaitvā vivaṭaina mukhaina anunāsikaṃ akatvā vuc‍cati|

tattha  ‘‘suṇātu mai’’ti vattabbai ta-kārassa tha-kāraṃ katvā ‘‘suṇāthu mai’’ti vacanaṃ sithilassa dhanitakaraṇaṃ nāma, tathā ‘‘pattakal‍laṃ aisā ñattī’’ti vattabbai ‘‘patthakal‍laṃ aisā ñatthī’’tiādivacanaṃ| ‘‘bhantai saṅghau’’ti vattabbai bha-kāragha-kārānaṃ ba-kāraga-kārai katvā ‘‘bantai saṃgau’’ti vacanaṃ dhanitassa sithilakaraṇaṃ nāma| ‘‘suṇātu mai’’ti vivaṭaina mukhaina vattabbai pana ‘‘suṇaṃtu mai’’ti vā ‘‘aisā ñattī’’ti vattabbai ‘‘aisaṃ ñattī’’ti vā avivaṭaina mukhaina ananunāsikaṃ katvā vacanaṃ vimuttassa niggahitavacanaṃ nāma| ‘‘pattakal‍la’’nti avivaṭaina mukhaina anunāsikaṃ katvā vattabbai ‘‘pattakal‍lā’’ti vivaṭaina mukhaina anunāsikaṃ akatvā vacanaṃ niggahitassa vimuttavacanaṃ nāma| iti sithilai kattabbai dhanitaṃ, dhanitai kattabbai sithilaṃ, vimuttai kattabbai niggahitaṃ, niggahitai kattabbai vimuttanti imāni cattāri byañ‍janāni antaukammavācāya kammaṃ dūsainti| aivaṃ vadantau hi añ‍ñasmiṃ akkharai vattabbai añ‍ñaṃ vadati, duruttaṃ karautīti vuc‍cati|

itaraisu pana dīgharassādīsu chasu byañ‍janaisu dīghaṭṭhānai dīghamaiva, rassaṭṭhānai rassamaivāti aivaṃ yathāṭhānai taṃ tadaiva akkharaṃ bhāsantaina anuk‍kamāgataṃ pavaiṇiṃ avināsaintaina kammavācā kātabbā| sacai pana aivaṃ akatvā dīghai vattabbai rassaṃ, rassai vā vattabbai dīghaṃ vadati, tathā garukai vattabbai lahukaṃ, lahukai vā vattabbai garukaṃ vadati, sambandhai vā pana vattabbai vavatthitaṃ, vavatthitai vā vattabbai sambandhaṃ vadati, aivaṃ vuttaipi kammavācā na kuppati| imāni hi cha byañ‍janāni kammaṃ na kaupainti| yaṃ pana suttantikatthairā ‘‘da-kārau ta-kāramāpaj‍jati, ta-kārau da-kāramāpaj‍jati, ca-kārau ja-kāramāpaj‍jati, ja-kārau ca-kāramāpaj‍jati, ya-kārau ka-kāramāpaj‍jati, ka-kārau ya-kāramāpaj‍jati, tasmā da-kārādīsu vattabbaisu ta-kārādivacanaṃ na virujjhatī’’ti vadanti, taṃ kammavācaṃ patvā na vaṭṭati| tasmā vinayadharaina naiva da-kārau ta-kārau kātabbau…pai॰… na ka-kārau ya-kārau| yathāpāḷiyā niruttiṃ saudhaitvā dasavidhāya byañ‍jananiruttiyā vuttadausai pariharantaina kammavācā kātabbā| itarathā hi sāvanaṃ hāpaiti nāma|

akālai vā sāvaitīti sāvanāya akālai anaukāsai ñattiṃ aṭṭhapaitvā paṭhamaṃyaiva anussāvanakammaṃ katvā pacchā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti| iti imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi anussāvanatau kammāni vipaj‍janti|
